Hard constraints: production-priority jobs must run <1 hour (iris --timeout 3500); 20 minutes max post-compile runtime (watchdog STEP_BUDGET=1200, COMPILE_CEILING=1800); no added quantization; loss trajectory must be equivalent; keeps must hot-swap into the live hero and restore from its checkpoints.      1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2	# Submit one hero EP64 arm for the 8753-mfu loop (research/mcwitt/8753-mfu-loop, #8753 head base).
     3	#
     4	# Protocol (fixed for every iteration so arms stay comparable; see DESIGN.md):
     5	#   - one NVL72 rack on cw-us-east-08a, PRODUCTION priority allowed with job runtime <1h
     6	#     (user 2026-08-28): ARM_TIMEOUT 3500s outer backstop, watchdog.sh COMPILE_CEILING=1800 +
     7	#     STEP_BUDGET=1200 inner caps (20-min post-compile cap)
     8	#   - restore from the live hero's step-30000 checkpoint, mixture data (trained-router routing);
     9	#     NUM_STEPS is the ABSOLUTE stop step (checkpoint step + window), never a relative count
    10	#   - no profiler, no checkpoints, no eval
    11	#   - no MARIN_PJRT_WHEEL overlay: the xla-fork base's pinned wheel runs ragged EP64 (validated
    12	#     by the t8684-* arms 2026-08-28); COORD_SKIP_JAX_FLOOR=1 keeps the coordinator's stock jax
    13	#   - JAX_COMPILATION_CACHE_DIR rotated per run id (clique-init deadlock dodge)
    14	#
    15	# usage: RID=<run-id> VERSION=<calver> [REPO=<worktree>] [EXTRA_LAUNCH_ARGS="..."] arm.sh
    16	set -euo pipefail
    17	
    18	: "${WANDB_API_KEY:?set WANDB_API_KEY}"
    19	RID="${RID:?set RID}"
    20	VERSION="${VERSION:?set VERSION -- bump it per arm or the artifact layer reuses the last run}"
    21	
    22	LOOP_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
    23	REPO="${REPO:-$(cd "${LOOP_DIR}/../.." && pwd)}"
    24	
    25	PRIORITY="${PRIORITY:-production}"
    26	ARM_TIMEOUT="${ARM_TIMEOUT:-3500}"
    27	NUM_STEPS="${NUM_STEPS:-30030}"
    28	TRAINING_DATA="${TRAINING_DATA:-mixture}"
    29	MASTER_PARAMS="${MASTER_PARAMS:-disabled}"
    30	MOE_IMPL="${MOE_IMPL:-ragged_all_to_all}"
    31	RESTORE_FROM="${RESTORE_FROM:-s3://marin-us-east-02a/marin/grug/hero-12d8b6f0-dee637/2026.08.19.2/checkpoints/step-30000}"
    32	RESTORE_ARGS=()
    33	if [ -n "$RESTORE_FROM" ]; then
    34	  RESTORE_ARGS=(--restore-from "$RESTORE_FROM")
    35	fi
    36	# The learning-rate schedule's length; fixed across arms so losses stay comparable.
    37	SCHEDULE_STEPS=4470000

     1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2	# Out-of-process runtime cap, early release, and anomaly alerting for one arm.
     3	#
     4	# Exits (each prints one WATCHDOG marker line; the harness alerts on process exit, so exiting
     5	# fast IS the alert):
     6	#   1. Step budget: cancel STEP_BUDGET seconds after the first step this watch observes. Compile
     7	#      time varies run to run; an arm that pays a slow compile still gets its full measurement
     8	#      window, but an arm whose change makes *steps* slow gets no extra rack time.
     9	#   2. Compile ceiling: cancel if no step has appeared COMPILE_CEILING seconds after submission.
    10	#   3. Early release: cancel as soon as W&B shows the last step the scoring window needs.
    11	#   4. Preemption or task failure: `job describe` publishes failures= and preemptions= counters.
    12	#      Either going nonzero invalidates the measurement (a rescheduled gang recompiles and the
    13	#      step timings straddle the gap), so cancel immediately instead of letting the retry burn
    14	#      the ceiling.
    15	#   5. Coordinator death before the train job exists (e.g. a config guard raising at dispatch):
    16	#      previously this burned the whole compile ceiling; now it exits within one poll.
    17	# Iris's own --timeout on the coord job is the outer backstop; it must cover
    18	# COMPILE_CEILING + STEP_BUDGET. This watchdog runs in a separate process so a wedged CLI or a
    19	# lost session cannot defeat it.
    20	#
    21	# State polls are cheap (one describe RPC), so they run every POLL=20s; the W&B step query is
    22	# heavier and runs every third poll.
    23	#
    24	# usage: RID=<run-id> [STEP_BUDGET=900] [COMPILE_CEILING=1200] [SCORE_MAX_STEP=19] watchdog.sh
    25	set -uo pipefail
    26	
    27	RID="${RID:?set RID}"
    28	STEP_BUDGET="${STEP_BUDGET:-900}"
    29	COMPILE_CEILING="${COMPILE_CEILING:-1200}"
    30	SCORE_MAX_STEP="${SCORE_MAX_STEP:-19}"
    31	POLL="${POLL:-20}"
    32	STEP_POLL_EVERY="${STEP_POLL_EVERY:-3}"
    33	
    34	LOOP_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
    35	REPO="$(cd "${LOOP_DIR}/../.." && pwd)"
    36	IRIS=(uv run iris --config lib/iris/config/marin.yaml)
    37	COORD="/mwittmann/${RID}-coord"
    38	TRAIN="${COORD}/grug-train-${RID}"
    39	start=$(date +%s)
    40	# A production submission may queue behind another job, so the compile ceiling starts when the
    41	# train job is first observed running, not at submission. QUEUE_CEILING bounds the pending phase.
    42	QUEUE_CEILING="${QUEUE_CEILING:-10800}"
    43	deadline=$(( start + QUEUE_CEILING ))
    44	run_seen=-1
    45	first_step=-1
    46	steps=-1
    47	tick=0
    48	
    49	cancel_arm() {  # cancel_arm <reason>
    50	  echo "$(date -u +%H:%M:%S) cancelling ${COORD}: $1"
    51	  # Cancel the train job by its own path as well as through the coordinator. Iris's --timeout may
    52	  # already have taken the coordinator down, and a cancel routed only through a dead parent is
    53	  # exactly how a 16-node child gets orphaned on the rack.
    54	  ( cd "$REPO" && timeout 300 "${IRIS[@]}" job cancel "$TRAIN" 2>&1 | tail -2 )
    55	  ( cd "$REPO" && timeout 300 "${IRIS[@]}" job cancel "$COORD" 2>&1 | tail -2 )
    56	}
    57	
    58	state_line_of() {  # state_line_of <job path> -> "state failures preemptions" (empty if absent)
    59	  ( cd "$REPO" && timeout 120 "${IRIS[@]}" job describe "$1" 2>/dev/null \
    60	      | sed -n 's/^State: \([a-z_]*\).*failures=\([0-9]*\).*preemptions=\([0-9]*\).*/\1 \2 \3/p' | head -1 )
    61	}
    62	
    63	max_step() {
    64	  ( cd "$REPO" && timeout 180 uv run --with wandb python - "$RID" <<'PY' 2>/dev/null
    65	import sys, wandb
    66	try:
    67	    run = wandb.Api().run(f"marin-community/marin_moe/{sys.argv[1]}")
    68	except Exception:
    69	    print(-2); raise SystemExit
    70	steps = [row.get("_step", -1) for row in run.scan_history(keys=["_step", "throughput/mfu"])]
    71	print(max(steps) if steps else -1)
    72	PY
    73	  )
    74	}
    75	
    76	while :; do
    77	  now=$(date +%s)
    78	  if [ "$now" -ge "$deadline" ]; then
    79	    if [ "${first_step:--1}" -ge 0 ] 2>/dev/null; then
    80	      cancel_arm "step budget ${STEP_BUDGET}s past first step reached"
    81	      echo "WATCHDOG hard-cap step-budget"
    82	    else
    83	      if [ "${run_seen:--1}" -ge 0 ]; then
    84	        cancel_arm "no step within the ${COMPILE_CEILING}s compile ceiling"
    85	        echo "WATCHDOG hard-cap compile-ceiling"
    86	      else
    87	        cancel_arm "still queued after ${QUEUE_CEILING}s"
    88	        echo "WATCHDOG hard-cap queue-ceiling"
    89	      fi
    90	    fi
    91	    exit 0
    92	  fi
    93	
    94	  read -r train_state train_failures train_preemptions <<<"$(state_line_of "$TRAIN")" || true
    95	  if [ -z "${train_state:-}" ]; then
    96	    # No train job yet: the coordinator is either still dispatching or died before dispatch.
    97	    read -r coord_state coord_failures coord_preemptions <<<"$(state_line_of "$COORD")" || true
    98	    case "${coord_state:-}" in
    99	      succeeded|killed|cancelled|*failed)
   100	        cancel_arm "coordinator ${coord_state} before the train job existed"
   101	        echo "WATCHDOG coord-terminal state=${coord_state} failures=${coord_failures:-?}"
   102	        exit 0;;
   103	    esac
   104	  fi
   105	  if [ "${run_seen:--1}" -lt 0 ] && [ "${train_state:-}" = "running" ]; then
   106	    # Iris can report a gang "running" while another job still holds the rack; the W&B run is
   107	    # only created once distributed init actually completes, so gate the compile clock on it.
   108	    probe="$(max_step)"
   109	    if [ "${probe:--2}" -ge -1 ] 2>/dev/null; then
   110	      run_seen=$now
   111	      deadline=$(( now + COMPILE_CEILING ))
   112	      echo "$(date -u +%H:%M:%S) gang up (wandb run exists), compile ceiling ${COMPILE_CEILING}s starts now"
   113	    fi
   114	  fi
   115	  if [ "${train_preemptions:-0}" -gt 0 ] 2>/dev/null; then
   116	    cancel_arm "train job preempted (preemptions=${train_preemptions})"
   117	    echo "WATCHDOG preempted train=${train_state} preemptions=${train_preemptions} step=${steps}"
   118	    exit 0
   119	  fi
   120	  if [ "${train_failures:-0}" -gt 0 ] 2>/dev/null; then
   121	    cancel_arm "train task failed (failures=${train_failures})"
   122	    echo "WATCHDOG task-failed train=${train_state} failures=${train_failures} step=${steps}"
   123	    exit 0
   124	  fi
   125	  case "${train_state:-}" in
   126	    succeeded|killed|cancelled|*failed)
   127	      echo "WATCHDOG terminal train=${train_state} step=${steps}"; exit 0;;
   128	  esac
   129	
   130	  if [ $(( tick % STEP_POLL_EVERY )) -eq 0 ]; then
   131	    steps="$(max_step)"
   132	    echo "$(date -u +%H:%M:%S) t=$(( now - start ))s train=${train_state:-none} step=${steps}"
   133	    # A restored arm resumes at the checkpoint's step, so progress is counted from the first step
   134	    # this watch actually observed rather than from zero.
   135	    if [ "${first_step:--1}" -lt 0 ] && [ "${steps:--1}" -ge 0 ] 2>/dev/null; then
   136	      first_step="$steps"
   137	      deadline=$(( now + STEP_BUDGET ))
   138	      echo "$(date -u +%H:%M:%S) first observed step=${first_step}, step budget ${STEP_BUDGET}s starts now"
   139	    fi
   140	    if [ "${first_step:--1}" -ge 0 ] 2>/dev/null && [ $(( steps - first_step )) -ge "$SCORE_MAX_STEP" ]; then
   141	      cancel_arm "scored through step ${steps} (${SCORE_MAX_STEP} past first)"
   142	      echo "WATCHDOG scored step=${steps} first=${first_step}"
   143	      exit 0
   144	    fi
   145	  fi
   146	  tick=$(( tick + 1 ))
   147	  sleep "$POLL"
   148	done

    58	
    59	DEFAULT_HERO_STEPS = 25
    60	HERO_CHECKPOINT_INTERVAL = timedelta(minutes=15)
    61	
    62	
    63	def build_diagnostic_run(
    64	    *,
    65	    run_id: str,
    66	    dp_racks: int,
    67	    num_steps: int,
    68	    schedule_steps: int | None = None,
    69	    seed: int = 0,
    70	    batch_size: int = HERO_EP_BATCH_SIZE,
    71	    num_experts: int | None = None,
    72	    num_experts_per_token: int | None = None,
    73	    intermediate_dim: int | None = None,
    74	    capacity_factor: float | None = None,
    75	    latent_dim: int | None = None,
    76	    moe_implementation: str | None = None,
    77	    master_param_mode: MasterParamMode = HERO_MASTER_PARAM_MODE,
    78	    processes_per_task: int = HERO_PROCESSES_PER_TASK,
    79	    eval_every: int = 0,
    80	    save_checkpoints: bool = False,
    81	    checkpoint_interval: timedelta = HERO_CHECKPOINT_INTERVAL,
    82	    checkpoint_path: str | None = None,
    83	    checkpoint_debug: CheckpointDebugConfig | None = None,
    84	    watch_interval: int = HERO_WATCH_INTERVAL,
    85	    watch_mode: WatchMode = WatchMode.INLINE,
    86	    profile_steps: int = 0,
    87	    profile_start_step: int = 5,
    88	    training_data_mode: TrainingDataMode = TrainingDataMode.MIXTURE,
    89	    version: str | None = None,
    90	) -> ArtifactStep[HeroThroughputResult]:
    91	    """Build a bounded diagnostic run for the production EP64 hero recipe.
    92	
    93	    The overrides sweep expert count, expert width, routed top-k, and routing capacity from the
    94	    hero spec. They keep the hidden dimension, so the compute-scaled optimizer values stay
    95	    comparable across a sweep. ``None`` keeps the hero value.
    96	
    97	    ``batch_size`` is the global batch across all data-parallel racks. It does not scale with
    98	    ``dp_racks``. ``batch_size`` and ``schedule_steps`` change the token budget for the heuristic.
    99	    """
   100	    if not run_id.strip():
   101	        raise ValueError("run_id must not be empty")
   102	    if dp_racks <= 0:
   103	        raise ValueError(f"dp_racks must be positive, got {dp_racks}")
   104	    if num_steps <= 0:
   105	        raise ValueError(f"num_steps must be positive, got {num_steps}")
   106	    if checkpoint_interval <= timedelta(0):
   107	        raise ValueError(f"checkpoint_interval must be positive, got {checkpoint_interval}")
   108	    if profile_steps < 0:
   109	        raise ValueError(f"profile_steps must be non-negative, got {profile_steps}")
   110	    if profile_start_step < 0:
   111	        raise ValueError(f"profile_start_step must be non-negative, got {profile_start_step}")
   112	    if profile_steps > 0 and profile_start_step >= num_steps:
   113	        raise ValueError(f"profile_start_step must be less than num_steps={num_steps}, got {profile_start_step}")
   114	    # `schedule_steps` sets the whole learning-rate schedule; `num_steps` is the absolute step the
   115	    # run stops at (a restore resumes mid-schedule, so it must lie past the restored step).
   116	    # Both matter, and they enter in different places. The optimizer heuristic scales learning rate,
   117	    # adam_lr, and epsilon from a token budget (`num_train_steps * batch * seq`), which fixes the
   118	    # peak. Warmup and decay are *fractions* of `TrainerConfig.num_train_steps`, so that field has to
   119	    # carry the schedule length too -- passing `num_steps` there warms up in `0.01 * num_steps` and
   120	    # decays to `min_lr_ratio` by the end of the short run, which is a whole miniature schedule
   121	    # rather than the head of a long one. Default keeps the two equal, which is the previous behavior.
   122	    if schedule_steps is not None and schedule_steps <= 0:
   123	        raise ValueError(f"schedule_steps must be positive, got {schedule_steps}")
   124	    if schedule_steps is not None and schedule_steps < num_steps:
   125	        raise ValueError(f"schedule_steps={schedule_steps} must be at least num_steps={num_steps}")
   126	    total_schedule_steps = schedule_steps if schedule_steps is not None else num_steps
   127	    _, optimizer = build_hero_configs(
   128	        num_train_steps=total_schedule_steps,
   129	        batch_size=batch_size,
   130	    )
   131	    model = HERO_MODEL_CONFIG
   132	    overrides = {
   133	        name: value
   134	        for name, value in (
   135	            ("num_experts", num_experts),
   136	            ("num_experts_per_token", num_experts_per_token),
   137	            ("intermediate_dim", intermediate_dim),
   138	            ("capacity_factor", capacity_factor),
   139	            ("latent_dim", latent_dim),
   140	            ("moe_implementation", moe_implementation),
   141	        )
   142	        if value is not None
   143	    }
   144	    if overrides:
   145	        model = dataclasses.replace(model, **overrides)
   146	    model = with_transport_remat_mode(model)
   147	    # A bank that is not divisible by the expert axis fails inside `moe_mlp`, which is after the rack
   148	    # is already allocated and the workspace is built. Reject it here instead.
   149	    if model.num_experts % HERO_EP_EXPERT_AXIS_SIZE != 0:
   150	        raise ValueError(f"num_experts={model.num_experts} must be divisible by {HERO_EP_EXPERT_AXIS_SIZE}")
   151	    local_experts = model.num_experts // HERO_EP_EXPERT_AXIS_SIZE
   152	    if local_experts % model.num_expert_waves != 0:
   153	        raise ValueError(
   154	            f"local expert count={local_experts} must be divisible by num_expert_waves={model.num_expert_waves}"
   155	        )
   156	    # The ragged transport requires one GPU per process; fail fast if this is not satisfied.
   157	    if model.moe_implementation == RAGGED_MOE_IMPLEMENTATION and processes_per_task != HERO_GPUS_PER_NODE:
   158	        raise ValueError(
   159	            f"{RAGGED_MOE_IMPLEMENTATION} needs one process per GPU: pass "
   160	            f"processes_per_task={HERO_GPUS_PER_NODE}, got {processes_per_task}"
   161	        )
   162	    pooled = model.moe_implementation == "fixed_pooled_wave_all_to_all"
   163	    if pooled and model.pooled_transport_capacity_factor is None:
   164	        raise AssertionError("the pooled-wave hero requires a transport capacity factor")
   165	    backend_tag = model.moe_implementation.replace("_", "-")
   166	    capacity_tag = f"capacity-{model.capacity_factor:g}"
   167	    # Only the pooled transport has a receiver capacity of its own to report.
   168	    transport_capacity_tags = (f"transport-capacity-{model.pooled_transport_capacity_factor:g}",) if pooled else ()
   169	    wave_tag = f"expert-waves-{model.num_expert_waves}"
   170	    size_tag = f"e{model.num_experts}-i{model.intermediate_dim}"
   171	    wandb_project = os.environ.get("WANDB_PROJECT") or DEFAULT_WANDB_PROJECT
   172	    grug_trainer = hero_grug_trainer_config(
   173	        replica_axis_size=dp_racks,
   174	        training_data_mode=training_data_mode,
   175	        watch_mode=watch_mode,
   176	        save_checkpoints=save_checkpoints,
   177	        master_param_mode=master_param_mode,
   178	    )
   179	    train_resources = ResourceConfig.with_gpu(
   180	        "GB200",
   181	        count=HERO_GPUS_PER_NODE,
   182	        cpu=HERO_NODE_CPU,
   183	        ram=HERO_NODE_RAM,
   184	        disk=HERO_NODE_DISK,
   185	        replicas=HERO_EP_NODES * dp_racks,
   186	    )
   187	    name = f"grug/{run_id}"
   188	    version = resolve_version(name, version)
   189	    validation = validation_datasets() if eval_every > 0 else []
   190	    flops_per_example, _ = _compute_flops(model_config=model)
   191	    experiment_flops = flops_per_example * batch_size * total_schedule_steps
   192	
   193	    def build_config(ctx: StepContext) -> GrugRunConfig:
   194	        trainer = hero_trainer_config(
   195	            run_id=run_id,
   196	            seed=seed,
   197	            train_batch_size=batch_size,
   198	            num_train_steps=total_schedule_steps,
   199	            master_param_mode=master_param_mode,
   200	            profiler=ProfilerConfig(
   201	                enabled=profile_steps > 0,
   202	                start_step=profile_start_step,
   203	                num_steps=profile_steps,
   204	                # One rank is enough for a step trace, and tracing all 64 multiplies the upload
   205	                # without adding signal.
   206	                process_index=0,
   207	                # Host scopes and HLO metadata identify compiled model regions in XProf.
   208	                profile_options=ProfileOptionsConfig(
   209	                    host_tracer_level=1,
   210	                    python_tracer_level=0,
   211	                    enable_hlo_proto=True,
   212	                ),
   213	            ),
   214	            tracker=WandbConfig(
   215	                entity="marin-community",
   216	                project=wandb_project,
   217	                tags=[
   218	                    "grug",
   219	                    "moe",
   220	                    "hero",
   221	                    "ep",
   222	                    backend_tag,
   223	                    capacity_tag,
   224	                    f"master-params-{master_param_mode.value.replace('_', '-')}",
   225	                    *transport_capacity_tags,
   226	                    wave_tag,
   227	                    size_tag,
   228	                    "gb200",
   229	                    HARRIER_MIX_2026_08_18_TAG,
   230	                    "MHEP",
   231	                ],
   232	                group="moe-hero-ep",
   233	                name=run_id,
   234	                replicate_path=ctx.output_path,
   235	            ),
   236	            watch=WatchConfig(interval=watch_interval),
   237	            # Levanter's default base path is pod-local, so a preempted run would have nothing to
   238	            # resume from. `checkpoint_path` overrides this for runs targeting disposable storage.
   239	            checkpointer=CheckpointerConfig(
   240	                base_path=checkpoint_path or prefix_join(ctx.output_path, "checkpoints"),
   241	                temporary_base_path=None,
   242	                save_interval=checkpoint_interval,
   243	                keep=None,
   244	                append_run_id_to_base_path=False,
   245	                delete_old_temp_checkpoints=True,
   246	                keep_last_temporary_checkpoints=1,
   247	                debug=checkpoint_debug or CheckpointDebugConfig(),
   248	            ),
   249	        )
   250	        data = harrier_mix_2026_08_18_data_config(
   251	            ctx=ctx,
   252	            total_steps=total_schedule_steps,
   253	            batch_size=batch_size,
   254	            max_seq_len=model.max_seq_len,
   255	            experiment_flops=experiment_flops,
   256	            validation=validation,
   257	        )
   258	        return GrugRunConfig(
   259	            model=model,
   260	            data=data,
   261	            resources=ctx.runtime_arg("train_resources"),
   262	            tensorstore_cache_bytes=HERO_TENSORSTORE_CACHE_BYTES,
   263	            optimizer=optimizer,
   264	            trainer=dataclasses.replace(grug_trainer, trainer=trainer),
   265	            # Off by default so a throughput run stays a throughput run. Turn it on to make a
   266	            # run scoreable: comparing configs needs held-out loss, not train loss.
   267	            eval=(
   268	                GrugEvalConfig(
   269	                    steps_per_eval=eval_every,
   270	                    eval_batch_size=HERO_EP_EXPERT_AXIS_SIZE * dp_racks,
   271	                    eval_ema=False,
   272	                    compute_bpb=True,
   273	                    dropless_eval=True,
   274	                )
   275	                if eval_every > 0
   276	                else None
   277	            ),
   278	            stop_after_steps=num_steps,
   279	            processes_per_task=processes_per_task,
   280	        )
   281	
   282	    return ArtifactStep(
   283	        name=user_namespaced_name(name, version),
   284	        version=version,
   285	        artifact_type=HeroThroughputResult,
   286	        run=run_grug,
   287	        build_config=build_config,
   288	        deps=(HARRIER_MIX_2026_08_18_STORE, *validation),
   289	        runtime_args={"train_resources": train_resources},
   290	    )

# Experiments Agent Notes

Start with `/AGENTS.md`; this file adds experiment-specific guidance.

## Dataset Catalogs

All dataset definitions live under `experiments/datasets/`, one leaf module per
dataset (or dataset family), built with the lazy data builders in
`marin.experiment.data` (`tokenized`, `hf_download`, `pretokenized`, ...).

- A single-corpus dataset exposes `<name>_dataset() -> ArtifactStep[TokenizedCache]`.
- A keyed family exposes `<name>_datasets() -> dict[str, ArtifactStep[TokenizedCache]]`,
  keyed by subset; a `<name>_dataset(key)` builder is added only when the keys are a
  natural registry (e.g. a Paloma subset).
- Mixture weights are policy, not catalog: keep them in a separate
  `<NAME>_MIXTURE_WEIGHTS` constant keyed the same way, and let the experiment pick
  weights and assemble them with `mixture()`.
- A module that builds tokenized-cache handles is runnable: end it with
  `if __name__ == "__main__": dataset_main(<family-dict>)`. `python -m
  experiments.datasets.<name>` prints the build plan; `--run` (optionally `--only
  <keys>`) builds it. Modules of a different shape — `eval` (builds `EvalDataset`s)
  and the `instruction`/`preference` HF-id registries — are exceptions with no CLI.
  Never change a handle's `name=`/`version=`/`pin=`/path strings — those are
  artifact identities.
- `__init__.py` stays docstring-only (no re-exports); import catalogs directly, e.g.
  `from experiments.datasets.nemotron import nemotron_datasets`.

## Artifact Path Rules

- Do not hardcode `gs://...` paths in experiment configs unless there is no viable alternative.
- Prefer artifact dependencies resolved at run time via `ctx.path(dep)` inside a recipe's `build_config`, so paths stay region-aware and reproducible.
- To reference a pre-existing external output, use `adopt(name, version, source=...)` (see `marin.execution.lazy`) instead of a raw `gs://` URL.
- If a referenced artifact may come from another region, point its source at a `mirror://...` path so reads are local-first and copied once when needed.

## Cross-Region Large Artifacts

- For `mirror://` copies, up to 10 GB can be copied without explicit human permission.
- If a `mirror://` copy would exceed 10 GB, get explicit human permission first, regardless of previous instructions.
- After approval, mirror it once into the local region and reference that mirrored copy thereafter.

## Mirror FS (Quick Reference)

- `mirror://` is an fsspec filesystem for transparent cross-region reads.
- Read behavior: check local `marin_prefix()` first; if missing, scan other Marin regional buckets, copy to local once, then serve locally.
- Copying uses a distributed lock so concurrent workers do not duplicate the same transfer.
- Write behavior: writes go directly to the local prefix.
- Both `mirror://` copies and direct cross-region GCS reads share a single process-global `TransferBudget` (default 10 GB) that raises `TransferBudgetExceeded` when exhausted.

Implementation references: `lib/iris/src/iris/marin_fs.py` and `rigging.filesystem` (the `TransferBudget`).

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	# Copyright The Marin Authors
     2	# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
     3	
     4	"""Score one arm of the ragged EP64 tuning loop from its W&B history.
     5	
     6	The metric is mean ``throughput/mfu`` over a fixed step window. The window is fixed for the whole
     7	loop: comparing arms scored over different windows compares warmup fractions, not transports.
     8	
     9	Also emits the two guard quantities that come from the same run -- the worst drop fraction and the
    10	loss at the window's last step -- so a faster arm that quietly dropped tokens or changed the model
    11	cannot be recorded as an improvement.
    12	
    13	usage: uv run --with wandb python score.py <run-id> [--lo 5] [--hi 19]
    14	"""
    15	
    16	import argparse
    17	import json
    18	import statistics
    19	
    20	import wandb
    21	
    22	MFU_KEY = "throughput/mfu"
    23	LOSS_KEY = "train/loss"
    24	DROP_KEY = "moe/drop_fraction"
    25	TOKENS_KEY = "throughput/tokens_per_second"
    26	
    27	
    28	def main() -> None:
    29	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    30	    parser.add_argument("run_id")
    31	    parser.add_argument("--lo", type=int, default=5, help="first scored step")
    32	    parser.add_argument("--hi", type=int, default=19, help="last scored step")
    33	    parser.add_argument("--project", default="marin-community/marin_moe")
    34	    parser.add_argument(
    35	        "--relative",
    36	        action="store_true",
    37	        help="Read lo/hi as offsets from the run's first logged step. A restored run resumes at "
    38	        "the checkpoint's step, so an absolute window would miss it entirely.",
    39	    )
    40	    args = parser.parse_args()
    41	
    42	    run = wandb.Api().run(f"{args.project}/{args.run_id}")
    43	    rows = list(run.scan_history())
    44	    steps = [int(r["_step"]) for r in rows if r.get("_step") is not None]
    45	    by_step: dict[int, dict] = {}
    46	    for row in rows:
    47	        step = row.get("_step")
    48	        if step is None:
    49	            continue
    50	        by_step.setdefault(int(step), {}).update({k: v for k, v in row.items() if v is not None})
    51	
    52	    first_step = min(by_step) if by_step else 0
    53	    lo, hi = (first_step + args.lo, first_step + args.hi) if args.relative else (args.lo, args.hi)
    54	
    55	    def series(key: str) -> list[float]:
    56	        return [float(by_step[s][key]) for s in sorted(by_step) if lo <= s <= hi and key in by_step[s]]
    57	
    58	    mfu = series(MFU_KEY)
    59	    drops = series(DROP_KEY)
    60	    losses = [(s, by_step[s][LOSS_KEY]) for s in sorted(by_step) if lo <= s <= hi and LOSS_KEY in by_step[s]]
    61	    tokens = series(TOKENS_KEY)
    62	
    63	    result = {
    64	        "run_id": args.run_id,
    65	        "state": run.state,
    66	        "max_step": max(steps) if steps else -1,
    67	        "window": [lo, hi],
    68	        "first_step": first_step,
    69	        "scored_points": len(mfu),
    70	        # A window with holes in it is not the window the loop agreed to compare, so say so loudly
    71	        # rather than averaging whatever happens to be there.
    72	        "window_complete": len(mfu) == hi - lo + 1,
    73	        "mfu_mean": round(statistics.fmean(mfu), 4) if mfu else None,
    74	        "mfu_stdev": round(statistics.stdev(mfu), 4) if len(mfu) > 1 else None,
    75	        "tokens_per_second_mean": round(statistics.fmean(tokens), 1) if tokens else None,
    76	        # Drops are an outcome here, not only a guard: the whole point of the ragged transport is
    77	        # moving tokens the pooled one clips, so a throughput win that costs drops is not a win.
    78	        "drop_fraction_max": max(drops) if drops else None,
    79	        "drop_fraction_mean": round(statistics.fmean(drops), 6) if drops else None,
    80	        "loss_last": float(losses[-1][1]) if losses else None,
    81	        "loss_last_step": losses[-1][0] if losses else None,
    82	    }
    83	    print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))
    84	
    85	
    86	if __name__ == "__main__":
    87	    main()

    42	  early release at +19 steps, cancel on preemption/failure/coord death;

    44	- Rack etiquette: hold/queue arms back-to-back while work is pending; do not
    45	  submit anything if the loop is blocked >1h on analysis.
    46	
    47	## Metric and guards
    48	
    49	- Metric: mean `throughput/mfu` over scored steps +5..+19 relative to the
    50	  first logged step (`score.py --relative`). Higher is better.
    51	- Guards (from the same run): `moe/drop_fraction` max/mean not worse than the
    52	  paired control; `train/loss` at the window's last step equivalent to
    53	  control (bf16-noise band; 24k arms showed 1.29964-65 across four arms);
    54	  `window_complete` must be true.
    55	- Noise model: placement variance across nights is ±2pp; KEEP decisions
    56	  require same-night interleaved control/treatment pairs (minimum 2 arms per
    57	  decision, prefer control-treatment-control-treatment when the delta is
    58	  small). A single-draw delta <0.15 MFU is treated as noise.
    59	- Fidelity gate for keeps: interleaved A/B window as above; any keep that
    60	  changes numerics beyond scheduling/layout gets flagged and needs the loss
    61	  guard plus explicit reasoning about why the trajectory is unchanged.

   108	
   109	## Decision rules
   110	
   111	- keep: same-night pair shows treatment > control by >0.15 MFU with guards
   112	  clean → commit stays, becomes the new control for later iterations.
   113	- discard: delta <= noise or guards fail → revert commit.
   114	- crash/invalid: watchdog kill, preemption, incomplete window → arm is
   115	  invalid, does not consume the hypothesis; resubmit once before discarding.
   116	- Every keep is re-verified implicitly by later same-night controls (the
   117	  control arm always runs the current kept stack).
   118	
   119	## Review protocol
   120	
   121	Before rack time each experiment's design gets adversarial review from (a) a
   122	fable agent and (b) `codex exec` CLI, both against the pinned worktree
   123	(`codex exec` needs stdin closed; `--base` incompatible with a custom
   124	prompt). Findings addressed or refuted in writing in the logbook before
   125	submission. Reviews of iteration N+1 overlap with rack time of iteration N.

     1	# Copyright The Marin Authors

     4	"""Bounded GB200 diagnostics for the production EP64 MoE hero recipe."""

    43	    hero_trainer_config,
    44	    validation_datasets,
    45	    with_transport_remat_mode,
    46	)
    47	from experiments.grug.moe_hero_ep.heuristic import build_hero_configs
    48	from experiments.grug.moe_hero_ep.train import (
    49	    RAGGED_MOE_IMPLEMENTATION,
    50	    GrugEvalConfig,
    51	    GrugRunConfig,
    52	    MasterParamMode,
    53	    TrainingDataMode,
    54	    WatchMode,
    55	    _compute_flops,
    56	    run_grug,
    57	)
    58	
    59	DEFAULT_HERO_STEPS = 25
    60	HERO_CHECKPOINT_INTERVAL = timedelta(minutes=15)
    61	
    62	
    63	def build_diagnostic_run(
    64	    *,
    65	    run_id: str,
    66	    dp_racks: int,
    67	    num_steps: int,
    68	    schedule_steps: int | None = None,
    69	    seed: int = 0,
    70	    batch_size: int = HERO_EP_BATCH_SIZE,
    71	    num_experts: int | None = None,
    72	    num_experts_per_token: int | None = None,
    73	    intermediate_dim: int | None = None,
    74	    capacity_factor: float | None = None,
    75	    latent_dim: int | None = None,
    76	    moe_implementation: str | None = None,
    77	    master_param_mode: MasterParamMode = HERO_MASTER_PARAM_MODE,
    78	    processes_per_task: int = HERO_PROCESSES_PER_TASK,
    79	    eval_every: int = 0,
    80	    save_checkpoints: bool = False,
    81	    checkpoint_interval: timedelta = HERO_CHECKPOINT_INTERVAL,
    82	    checkpoint_path: str | None = None,
    83	    checkpoint_debug: CheckpointDebugConfig | None = None,
    84	    watch_interval: int = HERO_WATCH_INTERVAL,
    85	    watch_mode: WatchMode = WatchMode.INLINE,
    86	    profile_steps: int = 0,
    87	    profile_start_step: int = 5,
    88	    training_data_mode: TrainingDataMode = TrainingDataMode.MIXTURE,
    89	    version: str | None = None,
    90	) -> ArtifactStep[HeroThroughputResult]:
    91	    """Build a bounded diagnostic run for the production EP64 hero recipe.
    92	
    93	    The overrides sweep expert count, expert width, routed top-k, and routing capacity from the
    94	    hero spec. They keep the hidden dimension, so the compute-scaled optimizer values stay
    95	    comparable across a sweep. ``None`` keeps the hero value.
    96	
    97	    ``batch_size`` is the global batch across all data-parallel racks. It does not scale with
    98	    ``dp_racks``. ``batch_size`` and ``schedule_steps`` change the token budget for the heuristic.
    99	    """
   100	    if not run_id.strip():
   101	        raise ValueError("run_id must not be empty")
   102	    if dp_racks <= 0:
   103	        raise ValueError(f"dp_racks must be positive, got {dp_racks}")
   104	    if num_steps <= 0:
   105	        raise ValueError(f"num_steps must be positive, got {num_steps}")
   106	    if checkpoint_interval <= timedelta(0):
   107	        raise ValueError(f"checkpoint_interval must be positive, got {checkpoint_interval}")
   108	    if profile_steps < 0:
   109	        raise ValueError(f"profile_steps must be non-negative, got {profile_steps}")
   110	    if profile_start_step < 0:
   111	        raise ValueError(f"profile_start_step must be non-negative, got {profile_start_step}")
   112	    if profile_steps > 0 and profile_start_step >= num_steps:
   113	        raise ValueError(f"profile_start_step must be less than num_steps={num_steps}, got {profile_start_step}")
   114	    # `schedule_steps` sets the whole learning-rate schedule; `num_steps` is the absolute step the
   115	    # run stops at (a restore resumes mid-schedule, so it must lie past the restored step).
   116	    # Both matter, and they enter in different places. The optimizer heuristic scales learning rate,
   117	    # adam_lr, and epsilon from a token budget (`num_train_steps * batch * seq`), which fixes the
   118	    # peak. Warmup and decay are *fractions* of `TrainerConfig.num_train_steps`, so that field has to
   119	    # carry the schedule length too -- passing `num_steps` there warms up in `0.01 * num_steps` and
   120	    # decays to `min_lr_ratio` by the end of the short run, which is a whole miniature schedule
   121	    # rather than the head of a long one. Default keeps the two equal, which is the previous behavior.
   122	    if schedule_steps is not None and schedule_steps <= 0:
   123	        raise ValueError(f"schedule_steps must be positive, got {schedule_steps}")
   124	    if schedule_steps is not None and schedule_steps < num_steps:
   125	        raise ValueError(f"schedule_steps={schedule_steps} must be at least num_steps={num_steps}")
   126	    total_schedule_steps = schedule_steps if schedule_steps is not None else num_steps
   127	    _, optimizer = build_hero_configs(
   128	        num_train_steps=total_schedule_steps,
   129	        batch_size=batch_size,
   130	    )
   131	    model = HERO_MODEL_CONFIG
   132	    overrides = {
   133	        name: value
   134	        for name, value in (
   135	            ("num_experts", num_experts),
   136	            ("num_experts_per_token", num_experts_per_token),
   137	            ("intermediate_dim", intermediate_dim),
   138	            ("capacity_factor", capacity_factor),
   139	            ("latent_dim", latent_dim),
   140	            ("moe_implementation", moe_implementation),
   141	        )
   142	        if value is not None
   143	    }
   144	    if overrides:
   145	        model = dataclasses.replace(model, **overrides)
   146	    model = with_transport_remat_mode(model)
   147	    # A bank that is not divisible by the expert axis fails inside `moe_mlp`, which is after the rack
   148	    # is already allocated and the workspace is built. Reject it here instead.
   149	    if model.num_experts % HERO_EP_EXPERT_AXIS_SIZE != 0:
   150	        raise ValueError(f"num_experts={model.num_experts} must be divisible by {HERO_EP_EXPERT_AXIS_SIZE}")
   151	    local_experts = model.num_experts // HERO_EP_EXPERT_AXIS_SIZE
   152	    if local_experts % model.num_expert_waves != 0:
   153	        raise ValueError(
   154	            f"local expert count={local_experts} must be divisible by num_expert_waves={model.num_expert_waves}"
   155	        )
   156	    # The ragged transport requires one GPU per process; fail fast if this is not satisfied.
   157	    if model.moe_implementation == RAGGED_MOE_IMPLEMENTATION and processes_per_task != HERO_GPUS_PER_NODE:
   158	        raise ValueError(
   159	            f"{RAGGED_MOE_IMPLEMENTATION} needs one process per GPU: pass "
   160	            f"processes_per_task={HERO_GPUS_PER_NODE}, got {processes_per_task}"
   161	        )
   162	    pooled = model.moe_implementation == "fixed_pooled_wave_all_to_all"
   163	    if pooled and model.pooled_transport_capacity_factor is None:
   164	        raise AssertionError("the pooled-wave hero requires a transport capacity factor")
   165	    backend_tag = model.moe_implementation.replace("_", "-")
   166	    capacity_tag = f"capacity-{model.capacity_factor:g}"
   167	    # Only the pooled transport has a receiver capacity of its own to report.
   168	    transport_capacity_tags = (f"transport-capacity-{model.pooled_transport_capacity_factor:g}",) if pooled else ()
   169	    wave_tag = f"expert-waves-{model.num_expert_waves}"
   170	    size_tag = f"e{model.num_experts}-i{model.intermediate_dim}"
   171	    wandb_project = os.environ.get("WANDB_PROJECT") or DEFAULT_WANDB_PROJECT
   172	    grug_trainer = hero_grug_trainer_config(
   173	        replica_axis_size=dp_racks,
   174	        training_data_mode=training_data_mode,
   175	        watch_mode=watch_mode,
   176	        save_checkpoints=save_checkpoints,
   177	        master_param_mode=master_param_mode,
   178	    )
   179	    train_resources = ResourceConfig.with_gpu(
   180	        "GB200",
   181	        count=HERO_GPUS_PER_NODE,
   182	        cpu=HERO_NODE_CPU,
   183	        ram=HERO_NODE_RAM,
   184	        disk=HERO_NODE_DISK,
   185	        replicas=HERO_EP_NODES * dp_racks,
   186	    )
   187	    name = f"grug/{run_id}"
   188	    version = resolve_version(name, version)
   189	    validation = validation_datasets() if eval_every > 0 else []
   190	    flops_per_example, _ = _compute_flops(model_config=model)
   191	    experiment_flops = flops_per_example * batch_size * total_schedule_steps
   192	
   193	    def build_config(ctx: StepContext) -> GrugRunConfig:
   194	        trainer = hero_trainer_config(
   195	            run_id=run_id,
   196	            seed=seed,
   197	            train_batch_size=batch_size,
   198	            num_train_steps=total_schedule_steps,
   199	            master_param_mode=master_param_mode,
   200	            profiler=ProfilerConfig(
   201	                enabled=profile_steps > 0,
   202	                start_step=profile_start_step,
   203	                num_steps=profile_steps,
   204	                # One rank is enough for a step trace, and tracing all 64 multiplies the upload
   205	                # without adding signal.
   206	                process_index=0,
   207	                # Host scopes and HLO metadata identify compiled model regions in XProf.
   208	                profile_options=ProfileOptionsConfig(
   209	                    host_tracer_level=1,
   210	                    python_tracer_level=0,
   211	                    enable_hlo_proto=True,
   212	                ),
   213	            ),
   214	            tracker=WandbConfig(
   215	                entity="marin-community",
   216	                project=wandb_project,
   217	                tags=[
   218	                    "grug",
   219	                    "moe",
   220	                    "hero",
   221	                    "ep",
   222	                    backend_tag,
   223	                    capacity_tag,
   224	                    f"master-params-{master_param_mode.value.replace('_', '-')}",
   225	                    *transport_capacity_tags,
   226	                    wave_tag,
   227	                    size_tag,
   228	                    "gb200",
   229	                    HARRIER_MIX_2026_08_18_TAG,
   230	                    "MHEP",
   231	                ],
   232	                group="moe-hero-ep",
   233	                name=run_id,
   234	                replicate_path=ctx.output_path,
   235	            ),
   236	            watch=WatchConfig(interval=watch_interval),
   237	            # Levanter's default base path is pod-local, so a preempted run would have nothing to
   238	            # resume from. `checkpoint_path` overrides this for runs targeting disposable storage.
   239	            checkpointer=CheckpointerConfig(
   240	                base_path=checkpoint_path or prefix_join(ctx.output_path, "checkpoints"),
   241	                temporary_base_path=None,
   242	                save_interval=checkpoint_interval,
   243	                keep=None,
   244	                append_run_id_to_base_path=False,
   245	                delete_old_temp_checkpoints=True,
   246	                keep_last_temporary_checkpoints=1,
   247	                debug=checkpoint_debug or CheckpointDebugConfig(),
   248	            ),
   249	        )
   250	        data = harrier_mix_2026_08_18_data_config(
   251	            ctx=ctx,
   252	            total_steps=total_schedule_steps,
   253	            batch_size=batch_size,
   254	            max_seq_len=model.max_seq_len,
   255	            experiment_flops=experiment_flops,
   256	            validation=validation,
   257	        )
   258	        return GrugRunConfig(
   259	            model=model,
   260	            data=data,
   261	            resources=ctx.runtime_arg("train_resources"),
   262	            tensorstore_cache_bytes=HERO_TENSORSTORE_CACHE_BYTES,
   263	            optimizer=optimizer,
   264	            trainer=dataclasses.replace(grug_trainer, trainer=trainer),
   265	            # Off by default so a throughput run stays a throughput run. Turn it on to make a
   266	            # run scoreable: comparing configs needs held-out loss, not train loss.
   267	            eval=(
   268	                GrugEvalConfig(
   269	                    steps_per_eval=eval_every,
   270	                    eval_batch_size=HERO_EP_EXPERT_AXIS_SIZE * dp_racks,
   271	                    eval_ema=False,
   272	                    compute_bpb=True,
   273	                    dropless_eval=True,
   274	                )
   275	                if eval_every > 0
   276	                else None

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	# Copyright The Marin Authors
     2	# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
     3	
     4	"""Click bootstrap for experiment ``main``s that defer their artifact versions.
     5	
     6	An experiment's driver constructs a handle graph and then either prints its plan or builds it.
     7	This module makes that a one-liner while wiring up the deferred-version machinery
     8	(:mod:`marin.execution.build_context`):
     9	
    10	- :func:`build_options` is a Click decorator that adds ``--version`` (the run-wide default),
    11	  ``--override NAME=VERSION`` (per-artifact, repeatable), ``--run`` (build vs. print the plan), and
    12	  ``--max-concurrent``. It builds the wrapped function's handle graph **inside** a
    13	  :class:`~marin.execution.build_context.BuildContext` assembled from those options, so any builder
    14	  that deferred its version (omitted ``version=``) resolves against them.
    15	- :func:`experiment_main` wraps a nullary ``build()`` into a ready-to-run command.
    16	
    17	``--version`` is required — there is no silent ``dev`` default, because a deferred *dataset* at a
    18	mutable version rebuilds its (often multi-TB) cache on every run and, unlike a training checkpoint,
    19	is not namespaced per user. To iterate, pass ``--version dev`` explicitly; the printed plan flags
    20	every artifact that resolved to a mutable version and will rebuild.
    21	
    22	An experiment with its own options composes with :func:`build_options` by stacking it *below* the
    23	experiment's own ``@click.option``s and ``@click.command()`` on top::
    24	
    25	    @click.command()
    26	    @click.option("--device", ...)
    27	    @build_options
    28	    def main(device):
    29	        return build(device=device)   # version now deferred to --version / --override
    30	
    31	    if __name__ == "__main__":
    32	        main()
    33	
    34	Construct the whole handle graph inside ``build()``; a builder invoked lazily after it returns has
    35	no active context and raises. Handles are frozen at construction, so reusing one across two
    36	invocations keeps its first resolved version.
    37	"""
    38	
    39	import functools
    40	from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping
    41	from typing import Any
    42	
    43	import click
    44	
    45	from marin.execution.artifact import is_mutable_version, validate_version
    46	from marin.execution.build_context import BuildContext, VersionCodex, build_context
    47	from marin.execution.lazy import ArtifactStep, lower, run
    48	
    49	BuildResult = ArtifactStep | Mapping[str, ArtifactStep] | list[ArtifactStep] | tuple[ArtifactStep, ...]
    50	
    51	
    52	def _version_callback(ctx: click.Context, param: click.Parameter, value: str) -> str:
    53	    """Reject a malformed ``--version`` at parse time, as a Click error rather than a traceback."""
    54	    try:
    55	        validate_version(value)
    56	    except ValueError as e:
    57	        raise click.BadParameter(str(e), ctx=ctx, param=param) from e
    58	    return value
    59	
    60	
    61	def _override_callback(ctx: click.Context, param: click.Parameter, value: tuple[str, ...]) -> dict[str, str]:
    62	    """Parse repeated ``NAME=VERSION`` into a validated ``{name: version}`` map, as Click errors."""
    63	    overrides: dict[str, str] = {}
    64	    for item in value:
    65	        name, sep, version = item.partition("=")
    66	        if not sep or not name:
    67	            raise click.BadParameter(f"{item!r} is not NAME=VERSION", ctx=ctx, param=param)
    68	        if name in overrides:
    69	            raise click.BadParameter(f"duplicate override for {name!r}", ctx=ctx, param=param)
    70	        try:
    71	            validate_version(version)
    72	        except ValueError as e:
    73	            raise click.BadParameter(f"override {name!r}: {e}", ctx=ctx, param=param) from e
    74	        overrides[name] = version
    75	    return overrides
    76	
    77	
    78	def _as_handles(result: BuildResult) -> list[ArtifactStep]:
    79	    """Normalize a ``build()`` return — one handle, a list/tuple, or a name→handle mapping."""
    80	    if isinstance(result, ArtifactStep):
    81	        return [result]
    82	    if isinstance(result, Mapping):
    83	        return list(result.values())
    84	    if isinstance(result, list | tuple):
    85	        return list(result)
    86	    raise TypeError(
    87	        f"build() must return an ArtifactStep, a list/tuple, or a mapping of them, not {type(result).__name__}"
    88	    )
    89	
    90	
    91	def _graph_handles(handles: list[ArtifactStep]) -> list[ArtifactStep]:
    92	    """Every handle reachable from ``handles`` (deps included), deduped by identity, deps first."""
    93	    seen: set[int] = set()
    94	    order: list[ArtifactStep] = []
    95	
    96	    def walk(handle: ArtifactStep) -> None:
    97	        if id(handle) in seen:
    98	            return
    99	        seen.add(id(handle))
   100	        for dep in handle.deps:
   101	            walk(dep)
   102	        order.append(handle)
   103	
   104	    for handle in handles:
   105	        walk(handle)
   106	    return order
   107	
   108	
   109	def _print_plan(handles: list[ArtifactStep]) -> None:
   110	    """Print the lowered plan, then a summary of every artifact's resolved ``name@version``.
   111	
   112	    The summary flags each artifact that resolved to a mutable version inline, since a mutable
   113	    version rebuilds on every run — the thing a driver most wants to catch before ``--run``.
   114	    """
   115	    for handle in handles:
   116	        click.echo(lower(handle))
   117	    click.echo("\nResolved versions:")
   118	    for handle in _graph_handles(handles):
   119	        flag = "  (mutable — rebuilds every run)" if is_mutable_version(handle.version) else ""
   120	        click.echo(f"  {handle.name}@{handle.version}{flag}")
   121	
   122	
   123	def build_options(fn: Callable[..., BuildResult]) -> Callable[..., None]:
   124	    """Add the shared version/run options to a command that returns the experiment's handle(s).
   125	
   126	    ``fn`` returns one :class:`~marin.execution.lazy.ArtifactStep`, a list/tuple of them, or a
   127	    name→handle mapping; it is called inside a :class:`~marin.execution.build_context.BuildContext`
   128	    built from ``--version`` and ``--override``. Without ``--run`` the lowered plan is printed;
   129	    with it the handles are built. An ``--override`` naming an artifact that never deferred (a typo,
   130	    or one that hardcodes its version) is rejected, so a silently-ignored override cannot slip a run
   131	    under the wrong version.
   132	    """
   133	
   134	    @click.option(
   135	        "--version",
   136	        "default_version",
   137	        required=True,
   138	        callback=_version_callback,
   139	        help="Run-wide default version for artifacts that defer (omit version=). "
   140	        "A calendar version YYYY.MM.DD, or 'dev' to iterate.",
   141	    )
   142	    @click.option(
   143	        "--override",
   144	        "overrides",
   145	        multiple=True,
   146	        metavar="NAME=VERSION",
   147	        callback=_override_callback,
   148	        help="Per-artifact version override, keyed by the artifact's name. Repeatable.",
   149	    )
   150	    @click.option("--run", "do_run", is_flag=True, help="Build the handles (default: print the plan).")
   151	    @click.option("--max-concurrent", type=int, default=8, show_default=True, help="Max steps built concurrently.")
   152	    @functools.wraps(fn)
   153	    def wrapper(
   154	        *args: Any, default_version: str, overrides: dict[str, str], do_run: bool, max_concurrent: int, **kwargs: Any
   155	    ) -> None:
   156	        codex = VersionCodex(default=default_version, overrides=overrides)
   157	        with build_context(BuildContext(versions=codex)):
   158	            handles = _as_handles(fn(*args, **kwargs))
   159	        unused = codex.unused_overrides()
   160	        if unused:
   161	            raise click.BadParameter(
   162	                f"never applied to any artifact: {sorted(unused)}. The name is a typo, or that "
   163	                "artifact hardcodes its version (an explicit version wins over an override).",
   164	                param_hint="--override",
   165	            )
   166	        if not do_run:
   167	            _print_plan(handles)
   168	            return
   169	        run(*handles, max_concurrent=max_concurrent)
   170	
   171	    return wrapper
   172	
   173	
   174	def experiment_main(build: Callable[[], BuildResult]) -> Callable[..., None]:
   175	    """Turn a nullary ``build()`` into a ready-to-run experiment command with the shared options.
   176	
   177	    ``experiment_main(build)()`` is the whole ``if __name__ == "__main__":`` body for an experiment
   178	    that takes no options of its own. For one that does, use :func:`build_options` directly (see the
   179	    module docstring for the stacking order).
   180	    """
   181	    return click.command()(build_options(build))
     1	# Copyright The Marin Authors
     2	# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
     3	
     4	"""Deferred artifact versions via an ambient build-time context.
     5	
     6	Every :class:`~marin.execution.lazy.ArtifactStep` is addressed by an explicit ``name@version``.
     7	Hardcoding a version on each builder call is tedious while iterating: a version is really a
     8	*run-wide policy* ("build everything at ``2026.07.16``, but pin this one dataset") that today has
     9	to be spelled out per artifact. This module lets a builder **defer** its version to a shared
    10	policy that rides on an ambient context:
    11	
    12	- A :class:`VersionCodex` maps an artifact ``name`` to a version — one ``default`` plus per-name
    13	  ``overrides``.
    14	- A :class:`BuildContext` carries the codex (and is the seam for future build-time properties).
    15	  It is set as an ambient :class:`~contextvars.ContextVar` while an experiment's handles are
    16	  *constructed*, via :func:`build_context`.
    17	- :func:`resolve_version` is what a builder calls: an explicit ``version=`` always wins; otherwise
    18	  the version comes from the ambient codex. A builder invoked with no explicit version and no
    19	  active context is an error — a deferred version never silently defaults.
    20	
    21	The version is resolved to a concrete string at *construction* time, before the handle is lowered.
    22	Nothing about identity changes: the fingerprint, the ``{name}/{version}`` path, and the drift check
    23	all see an ordinary version string, and resolving to the ``dev`` default reproduces today's
    24	mutable, per-user-namespaced behavior. The whole handle graph a driver builds must be materialized
    25	inside the ``with build_context(...)`` block — a builder (or a lazily-invoked factory) called after
    26	the block exits has no context to draw from. Construction is expected to be synchronous; a
    27	:class:`~contextvars.ContextVar` isolates concurrent async tasks and nests correctly, but does not
    28	propagate into freshly spawned OS threads.
    29	"""
    30	
    31	from collections.abc import Iterator, Mapping
    32	from contextlib import contextmanager
    33	from contextvars import ContextVar
    34	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    35	
    36	from marin.execution.artifact import validate_version
    37	
    38	
    39	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    40	class VersionCodex:
    41	    """Resolves an artifact ``name`` to a version: a ``default`` plus per-name ``overrides``.
    42	
    43	    ``overrides`` are keyed by the ``name`` passed to the builder (before any per-user
    44	    namespacing), matched exactly. Both the default and every override value are validated as
    45	    versions at construction, so a malformed ``--override`` fails before anything is built.
    46	    ``unused_overrides`` reports override names that no builder ever asked for — typically a typo,
    47	    or an override aimed at an artifact that hardcodes its version.
    48	    """
    49	
    50	    default: str
    51	    overrides: Mapping[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    52	    # Names actually consulted via version_for, for the unused-override check. Instrumentation
    53	    # only (compare=False, init=False); mutating the set on a frozen instance is allowed.
    54	    _used: set[str] = field(default_factory=set, compare=False, repr=False, init=False)
    55	
    56	    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
    57	        validate_version(self.default)
    58	        for name, version in self.overrides.items():
    59	            try:
    60	                validate_version(version)
    61	            except ValueError as e:
    62	                raise ValueError(f"override {name!r}: {e}") from e
    63	
    64	    def version_for(self, name: str) -> str:
    65	        """The version for artifact ``name``: its override if one is set, else the default."""
    66	        if name in self.overrides:
    67	            self._used.add(name)
    68	            return self.overrides[name]
    69	        return self.default
    70	
    71	    def unused_overrides(self) -> frozenset[str]:
    72	        """Override names never consulted since construction — a likely typo or a no-op override."""
    73	        return frozenset(self.overrides) - frozenset(self._used)
    74	
    75	
    76	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    77	class BuildContext:
    78	    """Ambient context that rides while an experiment's handles are constructed.
    79	
    80	    Carries the :class:`VersionCodex` today; the extension point for other build-time properties
    81	    (e.g. shared tags or a region hint) later, so those can ride the context instead of being
    82	    threaded through every builder.
    83	    """
    84	
    85	    versions: VersionCodex
    86	
    87	
    88	_ACTIVE: ContextVar["BuildContext | None"] = ContextVar("marin_build_context", default=None)
    89	
    90	
    91	@contextmanager
    92	def build_context(ctx: BuildContext) -> Iterator[BuildContext]:
    93	    """Make ``ctx`` the ambient build context for the duration of the ``with`` block.
    94	
    95	    Construct the experiment's handle graph inside the block so deferring builders can resolve
    96	    their versions from ``ctx``. Nests correctly and is restored on exit (including on exception).
    97	    """
    98	    token = _ACTIVE.set(ctx)
    99	    try:
   100	        yield ctx
   101	    finally:
   102	        _ACTIVE.reset(token)
   103	
   104	
   105	def current_build_context() -> BuildContext | None:
   106	    """The active :class:`BuildContext`, or ``None`` outside any :func:`build_context` block."""
   107	    return _ACTIVE.get()
   108	
   109	
   110	def resolve_version(name: str, version: str | None) -> str:
   111	    """The version for artifact ``name``: ``version`` if given, else the ambient codex's choice.
   112	
   113	    An explicit ``version`` always wins (deferral is opt-in per call). With no explicit version and
   114	    no active :class:`BuildContext`, this raises rather than guessing — a deferred version never
   115	    silently defaults.
   116	    """
   117	    if version is not None:
   118	        return version
   119	    ctx = current_build_context()
   120	    if ctx is None:
   121	        raise ValueError(
   122	            f"{name}: no version given and no active BuildContext. Pass version=..., or construct "
   123	            "this handle inside build_context(BuildContext(versions=VersionCodex(default=...)))."
   124	        )
   125	    return ctx.versions.version_for(name)

  1094	@job.command("cancel")
  1095	@click.argument("job_ids", nargs=-1, required=False)
  1096	@click.option(
  1097	    "--prefix/--exact",
  1098	    default=False,
  1099	    help="Match each Job ID as a prefix instead of exactly.",
  1100	)
  1101	@click.option("--stdin", is_flag=True, default=False, help="Read additional Job IDs from stdin CSV rows.")
  1102	@click.option("--dry-run", is_flag=True, default=False, help="Print the Jobs that would be cancelled.")
  1103	@click.pass_context
  1104	def cancel(ctx, job_ids: tuple[str, ...], prefix: bool, stdin: bool, dry_run: bool) -> None:
  1105	    """Cancel Jobs and their descendants."""
  1106	    targets = collect_resource_ids(job_ids, stdin)
  1107	    if not targets:
  1108	        raise click.UsageError("No Jobs given. Pass IDs or use --stdin.")
  1109	    if dry_run:
  1110	        if prefix:
  1111	            client = _remote_client(ctx)
  1112	            matches = [
  1113	                job_name.to_wire() for target in targets for job_name in client.active_job_names_for_prefix(target)
  1114	            ]
  1115	        else:
  1116	            matches = [JobName.from_wire(target).to_wire() for target in targets]
  1117	        click.echo(f"[dry-run] would cancel {len(matches)} Job(s):")
  1118	        for match in matches:
  1119	            click.echo(f"  {match}")
  1120	        return
  1121	    client = _remote_client(ctx)
  1122	    _print_cancelled(_cancel_jobs(client, tuple(targets), prefix))
  1123

  1125	@job.command("complete")
  1126	@click.argument("job_id")
  1127	@click.pass_context
  1128	def complete(ctx: click.Context, job_id: str) -> None:
  1129	    """Mark a Job and its unfinished descendants successful, then stop them."""
  1130	    job_name = JobName.from_wire(job_id)
  1131	    _remote_client(ctx).job(job_name).complete()
  1132	    click.echo(f"Completed job: {job_name}")

   349	    Set the resume behavior. Options: "allow", "must", "never", "auto" or None.
   350	    By default, if the new run has the same ID as a previous run, this run overwrites that data.

  1243	    def _commit_assignments(self, assignments: list[Assignment]) -> None:
  1244	        """Persist scheduler decisions to ``tasks.state = ASSIGNED`` rows.
  1245	
  1246	        Each :class:`Assignment` carries the effective priority band the backend
  1247	        computed against the snapshot's user spend, so ``assign_task`` stamps it
  1248	        onto ``tasks.priority_band``. The preemption pass then trusts that
  1249	        stamped value instead of recomputing from current spend every tick.
  1250	
  1251	        The next control tick's reconcile phase reads the ASSIGNED rows and fans
  1252	        out the Reconcile RPCs.
  1253	        """
  1254	        if self._config.dry_run:
  1255	            for assignment in assignments:
  1256	                logger.info("[DRY-RUN] Would assign task %s to worker %s", assignment.task_id, assignment.worker_id)
  1257	            return
  1258	        health = self.backend.health
  1259	        assert health is not None, "scheduling assignments produced by a backend with no liveness tracker"
  1260	        with self._db.transaction() as cur:
  1261	            ops.task.assign(cur, assignments, health=health)
  1262	
  1263	    def _apply_preemptions(self, preemptions: list[TerminalDecision]) -> None:
  1264	        """Finalize the backend's PREEMPT decisions.
  1265	
  1266	        Slice evictions for a coscheduled preemptor's N siblings are
  1267	        all-or-nothing. Victims stop on the next reconcile tick: the planner
  1268	        drops them from the worker's desired set.
  1269	        """
  1270	        if not preemptions:
  1271	            return
  1272	        if self._config.dry_run:
  1273	            for decision in preemptions:
  1274	                logger.info("[DRY-RUN] Would preempt task %s", decision.task_id)
  1275	            return
  1276	        with self._db.transaction() as cur:
  1277	            finalize(
  1278	                cur,
  1279	                preemptions,
  1280	                now=Timestamp.now(),
  1281	            )
  1282	        logger.info("Preemption pass: %d tasks preempted", len(preemptions))
  1283

  1285	        """Return cached scheduling diagnostic for a job, or None if unavailable."""
  1286	        return self._scheduling_diagnostics.get(job_wire_id)

  1288	    def _timeout_decisions(self, timeout_rows: Sequence[Row], now_ms: int) -> list[TerminalDecision]:
  1289	        """Turn execution-timeout rows from the snapshot into TIMEOUT decisions.
  1290	
  1291	        A row becomes a decision only once its attempt's
  1292	        ``started_at_ms + timeout_ms`` is already in the past.
  1293	        """
  1294	        decisions: list[TerminalDecision] = []
  1295	        for row in timeout_rows:
  1296	            if row.started_at_ms.epoch_ms() + int(row.timeout_ms) > now_ms:
  1297	                continue
  1298	            logger.warning("Task %s exceeded execution timeout, killing", row.task_id)
  1299	            decisions.append(
  1300	                TerminalDecision(
  1301	                    kind=TerminalKind.TIMEOUT,
  1302	                    task_id=row.task_id,
  1303	                    reason="Execution timeout exceeded",
  1304	                )
  1305	            )
  1306	        return decisions
  1307	
  1308	    def _mark_tasks_unschedulable(self, tasks: list[PendingTask]) -> None:
  1309	        """Mark a batch of tasks as unschedulable due to scheduling timeout.
  1310	
  1311	        Each entry must be a row from ``reads.pending_tasks_with_jobs``; it carries
  1312	        ``scheduling_timeout_ms`` so no secondary DB fetch is needed.
  1313	        """
  1314	        if not tasks:
  1315	            return
  1316	        if self._config.dry_run:
  1317	            for task in tasks:
  1318	                logger.info("[DRY-RUN] Would mark task %s as unschedulable", task.task_id)
  1319	            return
  1320	        with self._db.transaction() as cur:
  1321	            finalize(
  1322	                cur,
  1323	                self._unschedulable_decisions(tasks),
  1324	                now=Timestamp.now(),
  1325	            )
  1326	
  1327	    def _unschedulable_decisions(self, tasks: list[PendingTask]) -> list[TerminalDecision]:
  1328	        """Build UNSCHEDULABLE terminal decisions for scheduling-timeout tasks.
  1329	
  1330	        Each entry is a row from ``reads.pending_tasks_with_jobs`` carrying
  1331	        ``scheduling_timeout_ms``. Logs one warning per task.
  1332	        """
  1333	        decisions: list[TerminalDecision] = []
  1334	        for task in tasks:
  1335	            timeout_ms = task.scheduling_timeout_ms
  1336	            timeout = Duration.from_ms(timeout_ms) if timeout_ms is not None else None
  1337	            logger.warning(f"Task {task.task_id} exceeded scheduling timeout ({timeout}), marking as UNSCHEDULABLE")
  1338	            decisions.append(
  1339	                TerminalDecision(
  1340	                    kind=TerminalKind.UNSCHEDULABLE,
  1341	                    task_id=task.task_id,
  1342	                    reason=f"Scheduling timeout exceeded ({timeout})",
  1343	                )
  1344	            )
  1345	        return decisions

  1352	        """Promote PENDING->ASSIGNED for Kubernetes and ride the drain.
  1353	
  1354	        The dispatch drain is the single DB write a Kubernetes backend needs
  1355	        before reconcile (the controller owns the write; the backend places tasks
   320	    # no-op unless this job's root was received via handoff).

   336	def _apply_job_transition(
   337	    cur: Tx,
   338	    *,
   339	    job_id: JobName,
   340	    transition: Callable[[ReconcileState, Timestamp], ControllerEffects],
   341	) -> None:
   342	    """Apply one reconcile transition and remove endpoints for its subtree."""
   343	    now = Timestamp.now()
   344	    snapshot = load_closed_snapshot(cur, now=now, seed_job_ids=[job_id])
   345	    if job_id not in snapshot.job_configs:
   346	        return
   347	    effects = transition(ReconcileState.open(snapshot), now)
   348	    commit_effects(cur, effects)
   349	    subtree = [job_id, *snapshot.job_descendants[job_id].descendants]
   350	    cur.caches[EndpointsProjection].remove_by_job_ids(cur, subtree)
   351	
   352	
   353	def cancel(cur: Tx, *, job_id: JobName, reason: str) -> None:
   354	    """Cancel ``job_id`` and its descendant subtree through the kernel.
   355	
   356	    Loads a snapshot covering every job in the subtree and all their active
   357	    tasks (so the kernel can fire coscheduled-peer cascades on killed tasks),
   358	    runs :meth:`ReconcileState.cancel_job`, then applies the resulting effects.
   359	
   360	    Fixes the latent coscheduled-skip bug in the legacy direct-SQL cancel:
   361	    when one half of an atomic coscheduled group is cancelled, the kernel
   362	    cascades termination to the surviving peers instead of stranding them.
   363	    """
   364	    _apply_job_transition(
   365	        cur,
   366	        job_id=job_id,
   367	        transition=lambda state, now: state.cancel_job(job_id, reason, now),
   368	    )
   369	
   370	
   371	def complete(cur: Tx, *, job_id: JobName) -> None:
   372	    """Complete ``job_id`` successfully and stop its unfinished task attempts."""
   373	    _apply_job_transition(
   374	        cur,
   375	        job_id=job_id,
   376	        transition=lambda state, now: state.complete_job(job_id, now),
   377	    )
   378	
   379	
   380	def remove_finished(
   381	    cur: Tx,
   382	    job_id: JobName,
   383	    *,
   384	    record_tombstone: bool = True,
   385	) -> bool:
   386	    """Remove a finished job and its tasks from state.
   387	
   388	    Only removes jobs that are in a terminal state. Returns True if removed,
   389	    False if the job does not exist or is not finished. ``record_tombstone=False``
   390	    is for a federated resubmission replacing a finished run in place — the
   391	    parent must see the fresh submission's changelog row, not a tombstone.
   392	    """
   393	    job_state = reads.get_job_state(cur, job_id)
   394	    if job_state is None:
   395	        return False
   396	    if job_state not in TERMINAL_JOB_STATES:
   397	        return False
   398	    writes.delete_job(cur, job_id, record_tombstone=record_tombstone)
   399	    cur.register(
   400	        lambda: log_event(
   250	    for job_id, backend_id in pins:
   251	        by_backend.setdefault(backend_id, []).append(job_id)

   253	        for chunk in batched(job_ids, _ID_CHUNK_SIZE):
   254	            tx.execute(update(jobs_table).where(jobs_table.c.job_id.in_(chunk)).values(backend_id=backend_id))
   255	            tx.execute(update(tasks_table).where(tasks_table.c.job_id.in_(chunk)).values(backend_id=backend_id))

    98	            if wandb.run is None:

   100	                runx = wandb.init()
   101	                if runx is None:

   113	
   114	    # The prepare hooks do the full wandb-side conversion (SummaryStats expansion,
   115	    # wandb.Histogram construction) so the background worker only uploads. They are
   116	    # idempotent, so re-running them on an already-prepared payload is a no-op.
   117	
   118	    def _prepare_log(self, metrics):
   119	        return _convert_metrics_to_wandb_loggable(metrics)
   120	
   121	    def _prepare_summary(self, metrics):
   122	        return _convert_value_to_loggable_rec(metrics)
   123	
   124	    def _prepare_hyperparameters(self, hparams):
   125	        return _convert_value_to_loggable_rec(hparams)
   126	
   127	    def log_hyperparameters(self, hparams: dict[str, Any]):
   128	        if self._suppress_logging:
   129	            return
   130	        self.run.config.update(self._prepare_hyperparameters(hparams), allow_val_change=True)
   131	
   132	    def log(self, metrics: typing.Mapping[str, Any], *, step, commit=None):
   133	        if step is None and not commit:
   134	            step = self.run.step
   135	
   136	        if step < self._minimum_log_step:
   137	            if step - self._last_warning_step > 500:
   138	                logger.warning(
   139	                    f"Step {step} is less than the current step {self._minimum_log_step}. "
   140	                    "Cowardly refusing to log metrics."
   141	                )
   142	                self._last_warning_step = step
   143	            return
   144	
   145	        step = int(step)
   146	
   147	        to_log = self._prepare_log(metrics)
   148	
   149	        if self._suppress_logging:
   150	            return
   151	
   152	        if step < self.run.step:
   153	            if step - self._last_warning_step > 500:
   154	                logger.warning(
   155	                    f"Step {step} is less than the current step {self.run.step}. Cowardly refusing to log metrics."
   156	                )
   157	                self._last_warning_step = step
   158	            return
   159	
   160	        self.run.log(to_log, step=step, commit=commit)
   161	
   162	    def log_summary(self, metrics: typing.Mapping[str, Any]):
   163	        if self._suppress_logging:
   164	            return
   165	        self.run.summary.update(self._prepare_summary(metrics))
   166

    24	# Runtime-tuning env vars forwarded from the dispatcher to the train tasks.
    25	# Iris tasks don't inherit the submitter's shell, so anything the launcher was
    26	# given (e.g. `iris job run -e XLA_FLAGS ...`) must be re-exported explicitly.
    27	# JAX_PLATFORMS is excluded: the dispatcher runs CPU-only and its value must
    28	# not leak onto accelerator tasks.
    29	_FORWARDED_ENV_PREFIXES = ("XLA_", "LIBTPU_INIT_ARGS", "NCCL_", "JAX_", "MALLOC_")
    30	_FORWARDED_ENV_NAMES = ("LD_PRELOAD",)
    31	_FORWARDED_ENV_EXCLUDE = ("JAX_PLATFORMS",)
    32	
    33	
    34	def _forwarded_env_vars() -> dict[str, str]:
    35	    return {
    36	        k: v
    37	        for k, v in os.environ.items()
    38	        if (k in _FORWARDED_ENV_NAMES or k.startswith(_FORWARDED_ENV_PREFIXES)) and k not in _FORWARDED_ENV_EXCLUDE
    39	    }
    40	
    41	
    42	def _safe_job_suffix(run_id: str) -> str:
    43	    """Sanitize run IDs into Fray/Iris-safe job-name suffixes."""
    44	    return re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9_.-]+", "-", run_id)
    45	
    46	
    47	def dispatch_grug_training_run(
    48	    *,
    49	    run_id: str,
    50	    config: ConfigT,
    51	    local_entrypoint: Callable[[ConfigT], None],
    52	    resources: ResourceConfig,
    53	    max_retries_failure: int = 3,
    54	    max_task_failures: int = 10,
    55	    processes_per_task: int = 1,
    56	    priority: int = INHERIT_PRIORITY,
    57	) -> None:
    58	    """Submit a grug train entrypoint through Fray and wait for completion.
    59	
    60	    ``INHERIT_PRIORITY`` takes the submitting job's band, or ``interactive`` when the submitter is
    61	    not itself an Iris job -- which is the case for a launcher run from a dev box.
    62	
    63	    ``max_retries_failure`` is the per-task retry budget and ``max_task_failures`` is the
    64	    cumulative one. The job fails when either is exhausted, so raise the two together: a large
    65	    per-task budget under a small cumulative one still ends the job at the cumulative limit.
    66	    """
    67	    safe_run_id = _safe_job_suffix(run_id)
    68	    env_vars = resolve_training_env(base_env=_forwarded_env_vars(), resources=resources)
    69	    request = JobRequest(
    70	        name=f"grug-train-{safe_run_id}",
    71	        entrypoint=Entrypoint.from_callable(local_entrypoint, args=[config]),
    72	        resources=resources,
    73	        environment=create_environment(env_vars=env_vars, extras=extras_for_resources(resources)),
    74	        max_retries_failure=max_retries_failure,
    75	        max_task_failures=max_task_failures,
    76	        processes_per_task=processes_per_task,
    77	        priority=priority,
    78	    )
    79	    logger.info("Dispatching grug training via Fray: %s", request.name)
    80	    job = current_client().submit(request)
    81	    job.wait(raise_on_failure=True)
