   128	     1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
   129	     2	# Out-of-process runtime cap, early release, and anomaly alerting for one arm.
   130	     3	#
   131	     4	# Exits (each prints one WATCHDOG marker line; the harness alerts on process exit, so exiting
   132	     5	# fast IS the alert):
   133	     6	#   1. Step budget: cancel STEP_BUDGET seconds after the first step this watch observes. Compile
   134	     7	#      time varies run to run; an arm that pays a slow compile still gets its full measurement
   135	     8	#      window, but an arm whose change makes *steps* slow gets no extra rack time.
   136	     9	#   2. Compile ceiling: cancel if no step has appeared COMPILE_CEILING seconds after submission.
   137	    10	#   3. Early release: cancel as soon as W&B shows the last step the scoring window needs.
   138	    11	#   4. Preemption or task failure: `job describe` publishes failures= and preemptions= counters.
   139	    12	#      Either going nonzero invalidates the measurement (a rescheduled gang recompiles and the
   140	    13	#      step timings straddle the gap), so cancel immediately instead of letting the retry burn
   141	    14	#      the ceiling.
   142	    15	#   5. Coordinator death before the train job exists (e.g. a config guard raising at dispatch):
   143	    16	#      previously this burned the whole compile ceiling; now it exits within one poll.
   144	    17	# Iris's own --timeout on the coord job is the outer backstop; it must cover
   145	    18	# COMPILE_CEILING + STEP_BUDGET. This watchdog runs in a separate process so a wedged CLI or a
   146	    19	# lost session cannot defeat it.
   147	    20	#
   148	    21	# State polls are cheap (one describe RPC), so they run every POLL=20s; the W&B step query is
   149	    22	# heavier and runs every third poll.
   150	    23	#
   151	    24	# usage: RID=<run-id> [STEP_BUDGET=900] [COMPILE_CEILING=1200] [SCORE_MAX_STEP=19] watchdog.sh
   152	    25	set -uo pipefail
   153	    26	
   154	    27	RID="${RID:?set RID}"
   155	    28	STEP_BUDGET="${STEP_BUDGET:-900}"
   156	    29	COMPILE_CEILING="${COMPILE_CEILING:-1200}"
   157	    30	SCORE_MAX_STEP="${SCORE_MAX_STEP:-19}"
   158	    31	POLL="${POLL:-20}"
   159	    32	STEP_POLL_EVERY="${STEP_POLL_EVERY:-3}"
   160	    33	
   161	    34	LOOP_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
   162	    35	REPO="$(cd "${LOOP_DIR}/../.." && pwd)"
   163	    36	IRIS=(uv run iris --config lib/iris/config/marin.yaml)
   164	    37	COORD="/mwittmann/${RID}-coord"
   165	    38	TRAIN="${COORD}/grug-train-${RID}"
   166	    39	start=$(date +%s)
   167	    40	# A production submission may queue behind another job, so the compile ceiling starts when the
   168	    41	# train job is first observed running, not at submission. QUEUE_CEILING bounds the pending phase.
   169	    42	QUEUE_CEILING="${QUEUE_CEILING:-10800}"
   170	    43	deadline=$(( start + QUEUE_CEILING ))
   171	    44	run_seen=-1
   172	    45	first_step=-1
   173	    46	steps=-1
   174	    47	tick=0
   175	    48	
   176	    49	cancel_arm() {  # cancel_arm <reason>
   177	    50	  echo "$(date -u +%H:%M:%S) cancelling ${COORD}: $1"
   178	    51	  # Cancel the train job by its own path as well as through the coordinator. Iris's --timeout may
   179	    52	  # already have taken the coordinator down, and a cancel routed only through a dead parent is
   180	    53	  # exactly how a 16-node child gets orphaned on the rack.
   181	    54	  ( cd "$REPO" && timeout 300 "${IRIS[@]}" job cancel "$TRAIN" 2>&1 | tail -2 )
   182	    55	  ( cd "$REPO" && timeout 300 "${IRIS[@]}" job cancel "$COORD" 2>&1 | tail -2 )
   183	    56	}
   184	    57	
   185	    58	state_line_of() {  # state_line_of <job path> -> "state failures preemptions" (empty if absent)
   186	    59	  ( cd "$REPO" && timeout 120 "${IRIS[@]}" job describe "$1" 2>/dev/null \
   187	    60	      | sed -n 's/^State: \([a-z_]*\).*failures=\([0-9]*\).*preemptions=\([0-9]*\).*/\1 \2 \3/p' | head -1 )
   188	    61	}
   189	    62	
   190	    63	max_step() {
   191	    64	  ( cd "$REPO" && timeout 180 uv run --with wandb python - "$RID" <<'PY' 2>/dev/null
   192	    65	import sys, wandb
   193	    66	try:
   194	    67	    run = wandb.Api().run(f"marin-community/marin_moe/{sys.argv[1]}")
   195	    68	except Exception:
   196	    69	    print(-2); raise SystemExit
   197	    70	steps = [row.get("_step", -1) for row in run.scan_history(keys=["_step", "throughput/mfu"])]
   198	    71	print(max(steps) if steps else -1)
   199	    72	PY
   200	    73	  )
   201	    74	}
   202	    75	
   203	    76	while :; do
   204	    77	  now=$(date +%s)
   205	    78	  if [ "$now" -ge "$deadline" ]; then
   206	    79	    if [ "${first_step:--1}" -ge 0 ] 2>/dev/null; then
   207	    80	      cancel_arm "step budget ${STEP_BUDGET}s past first step reached"
   208	    81	      echo "WATCHDOG hard-cap step-budget"
   209	    82	    else
   210	    83	      if [ "${run_seen:--1}" -ge 0 ]; then
   211	    84	        cancel_arm "no step within the ${COMPILE_CEILING}s compile ceiling"
   212	    85	        echo "WATCHDOG hard-cap compile-ceiling"
   213	    86	      else
   214	    87	        cancel_arm "still queued after ${QUEUE_CEILING}s"
   215	    88	        echo "WATCHDOG hard-cap queue-ceiling"
   216	    89	      fi
   217	    90	    fi
   218	    91	    exit 0
   219	    92	  fi
   220	    93	
   221	    94	  read -r train_state train_failures train_preemptions <<<"$(state_line_of "$TRAIN")" || true
   222	    95	  if [ -z "${train_state:-}" ]; then
   223	    96	    # No train job yet: the coordinator is either still dispatching or died before dispatch.
   224	    97	    read -r coord_state coord_failures coord_preemptions <<<"$(state_line_of "$COORD")" || true
   225	    98	    case "${coord_state:-}" in
   226	    99	      succeeded|killed|cancelled|*failed)
   227	   100	        cancel_arm "coordinator ${coord_state} before the train job existed"
   228	   101	        echo "WATCHDOG coord-terminal state=${coord_state} failures=${coord_failures:-?}"
   229	   102	        exit 0;;
   230	   103	    esac
   231	   104	  fi
   232	   105	  if [ "${run_seen:--1}" -lt 0 ] && [ "${train_state:-}" = "running" ]; then
   233	   106	    # Iris can report a gang "running" while another job still holds the rack; the W&B run is
   234	   107	    # only created once distributed init actually completes, so gate the compile clock on it.
   235	   108	    probe="$(max_step)"
   236	   109	    if [ "${probe:--2}" -ge -1 ] 2>/dev/null; then
   237	   110	      run_seen=$now
   238	   111	      deadline=$(( now + COMPILE_CEILING ))
   239	   112	      echo "$(date -u +%H:%M:%S) gang up (wandb run exists), compile ceiling ${COMPILE_CEILING}s starts now"
   240	   113	    fi
   241	   114	  fi
   242	   115	  if [ "${train_preemptions:-0}" -gt 0 ] 2>/dev/null; then
   243	   116	    cancel_arm "train job preempted (preemptions=${train_preemptions})"
   244	   117	    echo "WATCHDOG preempted train=${train_state} preemptions=${train_preemptions} step=${steps}"
   245	   118	    exit 0
   246	   119	  fi
   247	   120	  if [ "${train_failures:-0}" -gt 0 ] 2>/dev/null; then
   248	   121	    cancel_arm "train task failed (failures=${train_failures})"
   249	   122	    echo "WATCHDOG task-failed train=${train_state} failures=${train_failures} step=${steps}"
   250	   123	    exit 0
   251	   124	  fi
   252	   125	  case "${train_state:-}" in
   253	   126	    succeeded|killed|cancelled|*failed)
   254	   127	      echo "WATCHDOG terminal train=${train_state} step=${steps}"; exit 0;;
   255	   128	  esac
   256	   129	
   257	   130	  if [ $(( tick % STEP_POLL_EVERY )) -eq 0 ]; then
   258	   131	    steps="$(max_step)"
   259	   132	    echo "$(date -u +%H:%M:%S) t=$(( now - start ))s train=${train_state:-none} step=${steps}"
   260	   133	    # A restored arm resumes at the checkpoint's step, so progress is counted from the first step

   650	def bulk_get_job_configs(tx: Tx, job_ids: Iterable[JobName]) -> dict[JobName, dict]:
   651	    """Return ``{job_id: config_dict}`` for all ``job_ids`` that have a config row.
   652	
   653	    Missing keys are silently absent. Uses a single IN-list query.
   654	    """
   655	    ids = list(job_ids)

   211	    rows = cur.execute(
   212	        select(jobs_table.c.job_id, jobs_table.c.num_tasks).where(
   213	            jobs_table.c.job_id.in_(bindparam("job_ids", expanding=True))
   214	        ),
   215	        {"job_ids": ids},
   216	    ).all()
   217	    return {row.job_id: int(row.num_tasks) for row in rows}

   219	
   220	def _build_job_configs(cur: Tx, job_ids: Iterable[JobName]) -> dict[JobName, JobConfigRow]:
   221	    """Build :class:`JobConfigRow` per job, folding in ``num_tasks``."""
   222	    ids = list(job_ids)
   223	    num_tasks_by_job = _load_job_num_tasks(cur, ids)
   224	    raw_configs = reads.bulk_get_job_configs(cur, ids)
   225	    return {
   226	        job_id: JobConfigRow(
   227	            job_id=job_id,
   228	            has_coscheduling=bool(config["has_coscheduling"]),
   229	            max_task_failures=int(config["max_task_failures"]),
   230	            preemption_policy=int(config["preemption_policy"]),
   231	            num_tasks=int(num_tasks_by_job.get(job_id, 0)),
   232	        )
   233	        for job_id, config in raw_configs.items()
   234	    }

   222	    def job_preemption_policy(self, job_id: JobName) -> int:
   223	        """Resolve the effective preemption policy.
   224	
   225	        Defaults: single-task jobs → ``TERMINATE_CHILDREN``, multi-task →
   226	        ``PRESERVE_CHILDREN``.
   227	        """
   228	        cfg = self._snapshot.job_configs.get(job_id)
   229	        if cfg is None:
   230	            return job_pb2.JOB_PREEMPTION_POLICY_TERMINATE_CHILDREN
   231	        policy = cfg.preemption_policy
   232	        if policy != job_pb2.JOB_PREEMPTION_POLICY_UNSPECIFIED:
   233	            return policy
   234	        if cfg.num_tasks <= 1:
   235	            return job_pb2.JOB_PREEMPTION_POLICY_TERMINATE_CHILDREN
   236	        return job_pb2.JOB_PREEMPTION_POLICY_PRESERVE_CHILDREN

    30	def xprof_viewer_url(service_url: str, profile_uri: str) -> str:
    31	    """Return the hosted XProf URL for an uploaded profile root."""
    32	    return f"{service_url.rstrip('/')}/open?{urlencode({'uri': profile_uri, 'tool': 'trace_viewer'})}"

    35	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    36	class ProfileOptionsConfig:
    37	    """Configuration forwarded to ``jax.profiler.ProfileOptions``."""
    38	
    39	    host_tracer_level: int | None = None
    40	    python_tracer_level: int | None = None
    41	    device_tracer_level: int | None = None
    42	    enable_hlo_proto: bool = False
    43	    include_dataset_ops: bool | None = None
    44	    advanced_configuration: dict[str, AdvancedProfileOptionValue] = field(default_factory=dict)
    45	
    46	    def build_jax_profile_options(self) -> jax.profiler.ProfileOptions:
    47	        options = jax.profiler.ProfileOptions()
    48	        if self.host_tracer_level is not None:
    49	            options.host_tracer_level = self.host_tracer_level
    50	        if self.python_tracer_level is not None:
    51	            options.python_tracer_level = self.python_tracer_level
    52	        options.enable_hlo_proto = self.enable_hlo_proto
    53	        if self.include_dataset_ops is not None:
    54	            options.include_dataset_ops = self.include_dataset_ops
    55	
    56	        advanced_configuration = dict(self.advanced_configuration)
    57	        if self.device_tracer_level is not None:
    58	            advanced_configuration["device_tracer_level"] = self.device_tracer_level
    59	        if advanced_configuration:
    60	            options.advanced_configuration = advanced_configuration
    61	        return options

    77	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    78	class ProfilerConfig:
    79	    """Configuration for scheduling the training profiler callback."""
    80	
    81	    enabled: bool = False
    82	    start_step: int = 5
    83	    num_steps: int = 25
    84	    perfetto_link: bool = False
    85	    create_perfetto_trace: bool = False
    86	    process_index: int | None = None
    87	    profile_options: ProfileOptionsConfig = field(default_factory=ProfileOptionsConfig)
    88	    upload: XprofUploadConfig = field(default_factory=XprofUploadConfig)
    89	
    90	    @property
    91	    def is_enabled(self) -> bool:
    92	        return self.enabled and self.num_steps > 0
    93	
    94	    def build_jax_profile_options(self) -> jax.profiler.ProfileOptions:
    95	        return self.profile_options.build_jax_profile_options()
    96	
    97	    def resolve_num_profile_steps(self, num_train_steps: int) -> int:
    98	        """Clamp profiling duration to the configured training length."""
    99	        total_prof_steps = self.num_steps
   100	        if total_prof_steps + self.start_step > num_train_steps:
   101	            logger.warning(
   102	                f"Adjusting profiler_total_steps from {total_prof_steps} to {num_train_steps - self.start_step}"
   103	            )
   104	            total_prof_steps = num_train_steps - self.start_step
   105	
   106	        return max(0, total_prof_steps)
   107	
   108	    def build(self, path: str, run_id: str, num_steps: int | None = None) -> Callable[[StepInfo], None]:
   109	        """Build the scheduled profiler callback."""
   110	        if num_steps is None:
   111	            num_steps = self.num_steps
   112	        upload_uri = self.upload.destination_for_run(run_id) if self.upload.enabled else None
   113	        if upload_uri is not None and not _is_xprof_ttl_root(StoragePath(upload_uri)):
   114	            logger.info("MARIN_PREFIX has no remote XProf TTL store; keeping the profile at %s", path)
   115	            upload_uri = None
   116	        service_url = None
   117	        if upload_uri is not None and StoragePath(upload_uri).scheme in ("gs", "s3"):
   118	            service_url = self.upload.service_url
   119	        return profile(
   120	            path,
   121	            start_step=self.start_step,
   122	            num_steps=num_steps,
   123	            create_perfetto_link=self.perfetto_link,
   124	            create_perfetto_trace=self.create_perfetto_trace,
   125	            profiler_options=self.build_jax_profile_options(),
   126	            process_index=self.process_index,
   127	            upload_uri=upload_uri,
   128	            xprof_service_url=service_url,
   129	        )
   130	
   131	
   132	def _is_xprof_ttl_root(path: StoragePath) -> bool:
   133	    if path.scheme not in ("gs", "s3") or not path.bucket:
   134	        return False
   135	    # Leave room after the TTL segment for xprof and at least one run segment.
   136	    return any(
   137	        _XPROF_TTL_SEGMENT.fullmatch(segment) and path.segments[index + 1] == "xprof"
   138	        for index, segment in enumerate(path.segments[:-2])
   139	    )
   140	
   141	
   142	def profile(
   143	    path: str,
   144	    start_step: int,
   145	    num_steps: int,
   146	    create_perfetto_link: bool,
   147	    create_perfetto_trace: bool = False,
   148	    profiler_options: jax.profiler.ProfileOptions | None = None,
   149	    process_index: int | None = None,
   150	    upload_uri: str | None = None,
   151	    xprof_service_url: str | None = None,
   152	) -> Callable[[StepInfo], None]:
   153	    """Schedule a JAX XPlane capture."""
   154	    profile_path = StoragePath(path)
   155	    if not profile_path.is_local:
   156	        raise ValueError(f"JAX profiler capture path must be local, got {path}")
   157	
   158	    local_path = Path(path)
   159	    local_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   160	    profile_window_started = False
   161	    trace_started = False
   162	    existing_sessions: set[Path] = set()
   163	
   164	    def is_tracing_process() -> bool:
   165	        return process_index is None or jax.process_index() == process_index
   166	
   167	    def profiler_callback_fn(step: StepInfo, *, force: bool = False):
   168	        nonlocal existing_sessions, profile_window_started, trace_started
   169	        if force and profile_window_started:
   170	            _stop_profile(max(start_step, step.step + 1))
   171	            return
   172	
   173	        # -1 b/c step is the finished step
   174	        if step.step == start_step - 1:

   446	def _reshard_tree_to_mesh(tree, mesh: Mesh):
   447	    """Move each array leaf onto ``mesh``, preserving its PartitionSpec.
   448	
   449	    The train and eval meshes name the same axes (only the ``expert``/``data`` sizes differ), so a
   450	    leaf's PartitionSpec is valid on both; ``jax.device_put`` performs the cross-mesh transfer. The
   451	    model's own ``reshard`` calls fix the exact layout inside the forward, so any valid placement on
   452	    the target mesh suffices here. Non-array leaves pass through.
   453	    """
   454	
   455	    def move(leaf):
   456	        if not isinstance(leaf, jax.Array):
   457	            return leaf
   458	        spec = leaf.sharding.spec if isinstance(leaf.sharding, NamedSharding) else P()
   459	        return jax.device_put(leaf, NamedSharding(mesh, spec))
   460	
   461	    return jax.tree.map(move, tree)
   462	
   463	
   464	def _to_dropless_local(
   465	    model: Transformer, *, implementation: MoeImplementation = DEFAULT_DROPLESS_MOE_IMPLEMENTATION
   466	) -> Transformer:
   467	    """Swap the scanned block's MoE expert backend to the selected dropless local path.
   468	
   469	    ``implementation``/``expert_chunks`` are static fields shared across the whole stacked block,
   470	    so one replacement covers every layer. The forward reads ``self.expert_mlp.implementation``
   471	    (not the model config), so this alone routes the eval dropless. Must run on an expert-collapsed
   472	    mesh: the local backend raises when the mesh expert axis is larger than one.
   473	    """
   474	    expert_mlp = model.stacked_blocks.stacked.mlp.expert_mlp
   475	    dropless = dataclasses.replace(expert_mlp, implementation=implementation, expert_chunks=1)
   476	    return eqx.tree_at(lambda m: m.stacked_blocks.stacked.mlp.expert_mlp, model, dropless)
   477	
   478	
   479	def _first_step_only(hook: Callable[..., None]) -> Callable[..., None]:
   480	    """Wrap ``hook`` so that it runs one time only, after the first optimization step.
   481	
   482	    ``StateCallbackRunner`` dispatches on ``next_step % every``, thus ``every=1`` is the only
   483	    interval that covers the first step. The gate makes that registration one-shot. A resumed run
   484	    starts above step 1 and never fires it.
   485	    """
   486	
   487	    # `LambdaCallback` reads the signature to decide whether to pass `force`, and the `**kwargs`
   488	    # below would otherwise advertise a `force` parameter that the wrapped hook can lack.
   489	    @functools.wraps(hook)
   490	    def gated(step, *args, **kwargs):
   491	        if step.next_step != 1:
   492	            return
   493	        hook(step, *args, **kwargs)
   494	
   495	    return gated
   496

   195	
   196	def _apply_hero_ep_runtime_defaults(
   197	    *,
   198	    inline_watch_enabled: bool,
   199	    moe_implementation: MoeImplementation | None,
   200	    remat_mode: RematMode,
   201	    processes_per_task: int = 1,
   202	) -> None:
   203	    env_defaults = dict(HERO_EP_RUNTIME_ENV)
   204	    if processes_per_task > 1:
   205	        # With one process per GPU, the per-process CUPTI sessions collide with each
   206	        # other and with CoreWeave's DCGM, so PGLE cannot profile and its recompile
   207	        # machinery only adds failure modes. Default it off; an explicit env wins.
   208	        env_defaults["JAX_ENABLE_PGLE"] = "false"
   209	    for name, value in env_defaults.items():
   210	        os.environ.setdefault(name, value)
   211	    xla_flags = os.environ.get("XLA_FLAGS", "").split()
   212	    ragged = moe_implementation == RAGGED_MOE_IMPLEMENTATION
   213	    if ragged:
   214	        overlap_limit = RAGGED_COLLECTIVE_OVERLAP_LIMIT
   215	    elif inline_watch_enabled:
   216	        overlap_limit = INLINE_WATCH_COLLECTIVE_OVERLAP_LIMIT
   217	    else:
   218	        overlap_limit = DEFAULT_COLLECTIVE_OVERLAP_LIMIT
   219	    # The scheduler's longer buffer live ranges fit on the ragged transport only once the layer
   220	    # carry leaves HBM. Without that offload its first-step NCCL allocations fail.
   221	    latency_hiding = not ragged or remat_mode == "offload_carry"
   222	    flag_defaults = (
   223	        f"{XLA_COLLECTIVE_OVERLAP_FLAG}={overlap_limit}",
   224	        f"--xla_gpu_enable_latency_hiding_scheduler={'true' if latency_hiding else 'false'}",
   225	        # The scheduler sizes the single `jit_train_step` temp arena against this percentage of
   226	        # its memory budget, roughly `133.6 GiB x percentage`. The pool holds 138.2 GiB and
   227	        # persistent state occupies 18.1 GiB of it, so an arena above 120.2 GiB cannot be served
   228	        # from pool free space and forces a fresh mapping against the ~17 GiB of physical memory
   229	        # outside the pool. The default 95 asks for 125.7 GiB and fails that way. 85 sizes the
   230	        # arena at 113.6 GiB, leaving enough slack for per-node variation in fragmentation. A
   231	        # lower percentage costs throughput, because a smaller arena makes `HloRematerialization`
   232	        # recompute more of the step.
   233	        "--xla_gpu_memory_limit_slop_factor=85",
   234	        XLA_DISABLE_GPU_COMMAND_BUFFER_FLAG,
   235	    )
   236	    explicit_names = {flag.partition("=")[0] for flag in xla_flags}
   237	    xla_flags.extend(flag for flag in flag_defaults if flag.partition("=")[0] not in explicit_names)
   238	    if remat_mode == "offload_carry":
   239	        # Not overridable, unlike the defaults above, including the scheduler flag it pairs with.
   240	        # Both flags can be inherited from another recipe, but only this one fails silently: a
   241	        # wrong overlap limit corrupts training, while an inherited scheduler setting either
   242	        # aborts in NCCL's first-step allocation and names itself, or runs the slower posture.
   243	        xla_flags = [f for f in xla_flags if f.partition("=")[0] != XLA_COLLECTIVE_OVERLAP_FLAG]
   244	        xla_flags.append(f"{XLA_COLLECTIVE_OVERLAP_FLAG}={OFFLOAD_CARRY_COLLECTIVE_OVERLAP_LIMIT}")
   245	    if ragged:
   246	        # Unlike the defaults above, these are not overridable. Selecting the host-launched
   247	        # one-shot kernel needs both flags cleared together plus a splits-per-peer count this
   248	        # branch no longer carries, so honoring a partial override would run a configuration
   249	        # nothing here measures. Drop any conflicting entry rather than relying on which
   250	        # occurrence XLA's parser keeps.
   251	        xla_flags = [f for f in xla_flags if f.partition("=")[0] not in _RAGGED_REQUIRED_XLA_FLAG_NAMES]
   252	        xla_flags.extend(RAGGED_REQUIRED_XLA_FLAGS)
   253	    os.environ["XLA_FLAGS"] = " ".join(xla_flags)
   254

   256	def verify_ragged_pjrt() -> None:
   257	    """Raise unless this process runs Marin's patched GPU PJRT plugin."""

   260	    except importlib.metadata.PackageNotFoundError as missing:
   261	        raise RuntimeError(
   262	            f"{PJRT_DISTRIBUTION} is not installed, so this process has no GPU PJRT plugin at all."
   263	        ) from missing
   264	    expected_prefix = f"{jax.__version__}+marin."
   265	    if not installed.startswith(expected_prefix):
   210	_gather_dispatch_rows.defvjp(_gather_dispatch_rows_fwd, _gather_dispatch_rows_bwd)

   212	
   213	def _moe_mlp_ep_ragged_a2a_local(
   214	    x_local: Float[Array, "Tlocal H"],
   215	    selected_experts_local: Int[Array, "Tlocal K"],
   216	    combine_weights_local: Float[Array, "Tlocal K"],
   217	    moe_w13_local: Float[Array, "Elocal H I2"],
   218	    moe_w2_local: Float[Array, "Elocal I H"],
   219	    *,
   220	    activation_fn: Callable[[jax.Array], jax.Array],
   221	    num_experts: int,
   222	    capacity_factor: float,
   223	) -> tuple[Float[Array, "Tlocal H"], CapacityOverflow]:
   224	    local_experts = moe_w13_local.shape[0]
   225	    if num_experts % local_experts != 0:
   226	        raise ValueError(
   227	            f"num_experts={num_experts} must be divisible by local expert count={local_experts} in EP mode"
   228	        )
   229	
   230	    shard_id = jax.lax.axis_index("expert")
   231	    ep_size = num_experts // local_experts
   232	    tokens_per_shard = x_local.shape[0]
   233	    topk = selected_experts_local.shape[1]
   234	    assignments_per_shard = tokens_per_shard * topk
   235	    local_capacity = int(math.ceil(capacity_factor * assignments_per_shard))
   236	    local_capacity = max(local_experts, local_capacity)
   237	
   238	    # Local experts are processed in sequential chunks so only one chunk's transport buffers
   239	    # are live at a time. The a2a outputs cannot be rematerialized (XLA never recomputes
   240	    # collectives), so unchunked they pin [capacity, H] + [TK, H] per block window and the
   241	    # hero step no longer fits next to NCCL's pools. Capacity splits evenly across chunks,
   242	    # which also makes drop clipping per-chunk.
   243	    chunks = _EXPERT_CHUNKS if local_experts % _EXPERT_CHUNKS == 0 and _EXPERT_CHUNKS > 1 else 1
   244	    chunk_experts = local_experts // chunks
   245	    chunk_capacity = max(chunk_experts, int(math.ceil(local_capacity / chunks)))
   246	    hidden_dim = x_local.shape[1]
   247	
   248	    with jax.named_scope("dispatch"):
   249	        flat_selected = selected_experts_local.reshape(-1)  # [TK]
   250	        sorted_indices = jnp.argsort(flat_selected)  # [TK]
   251	        group_sizes = jnp.bincount(flat_selected, length=num_experts).astype(jnp.int32)  # [E]
   252	        sorted_x = _gather_dispatch_rows(x_local, sorted_indices, topk)  # [TK, H]
   253	        all_group_sizes = jax.lax.all_gather(group_sizes, "expert")  # [S, E]
   254	
   255	    expert_mlp = _select_expert_mlp(activation_fn)

  1240	
  1241	    levanter.tracker.current_tracker().finish()

  1243	
  1244	def run_grug(config: GrugRunConfig) -> None:
  1245	    """Dispatch grug training through Fray jobs."""
  1246	    trainer = config.trainer.trainer
  1247	    if trainer.id is None:
  1248	        raise ValueError("trainer.id must be set before dispatching grug training.")
  1249	
  1250	    # Dispatch snapshots os.environ for the child task, so apply the hero defaults first.
  1251	    inline_watch_enabled = trainer.watch.is_enabled and config.trainer.watch_mode == WatchMode.INLINE
  1252	    _apply_hero_ep_runtime_defaults(
  1253	        inline_watch_enabled=inline_watch_enabled,
  1254	        processes_per_task=config.processes_per_task,
  1255	        moe_implementation=config.model.moe_implementation,
  1256	        remat_mode=config.model.remat_mode,
  1257	    )
  1258	    dispatch_grug_training_run(
  1259	        run_id=trainer.id,
  1260	        config=config,
  1261	        local_entrypoint=_run_grug_local,
  1262	        resources=config.resources,
  1263	        processes_per_task=config.processes_per_task,
  1264	        max_retries_failure=config.max_retries_failure,
  1265	        max_task_failures=config.max_task_failures,
  1266	    )

    75	    "XLA_PYTHON_CLIENT_ALLOCATOR": "cuda_async",
    76	    # `cuda_async` reads this fraction as the mempool release threshold, and PJRT sizes the
    77	    # collective pool at the remaining `(1 - fraction) x 184.3 GiB`. 0.75 sets a 138.2 GiB
    78	    # threshold and leaves ~46 GiB for collectives, above the ~28.5 GiB NCCL, cuBLAS, and the
    79	    # CUDA context hold outside the allocator. A higher fraction strands memory the step never
    80	    # uses, because the startup preallocation probe commits the whole threshold at once: 0.83
    81	    # pins 153.0 GiB in the pool and leaves under 3 GiB free on the device, so the arena
    82	    # allocation below has no room to remap into.
    83	    "XLA_PYTHON_CLIENT_MEM_FRACTION": "0.75",
    84	}
    85	XLA_COLLECTIVE_OVERLAP_FLAG = "--xla_gpu_experimental_parallel_collective_overlap_limit"
    86	DEFAULT_COLLECTIVE_OVERLAP_LIMIT = 4
    87	DEFAULT_DROPLESS_MOE_IMPLEMENTATION: MoeImplementation = "sonic_cute"
    88	# Full inline norm watch failed with overlap 4. Overlap 1 completed the selected full-watch gate.
    89	INLINE_WATCH_COLLECTIVE_OVERLAP_LIMIT = 1
    90	# The ragged transport wants the opposite scheduling posture from the fixed and pooled ones. Its
    91	# dispatch and combine form one long dependent chain, so admitting several concurrent collectives
    92	# only contends for the SMs the transport itself needs.
    93	RAGGED_COLLECTIVE_OVERLAP_LIMIT = 1
    94	# The layer-carry offload, the latency-hiding scheduler, and an overlap limit above 1 corrupt
    95	# training together, and no pair of the three does: the reloaded residual races its consumer in
    96	# backward, so a restored run reports a first-step loss near 9.5 instead of its checkpoint's
    97	# (#8317). Overlap 4 gained 0.07 MFU, so the offload forces this value rather than defaulting it.
    98	OFFLOAD_CARRY_COLLECTIVE_OVERLAP_LIMIT = 1

   109	    """Where a watched training step computes gradient and parameter statistics."""

   195	
   196	def _apply_hero_ep_runtime_defaults(
   197	    *,
   198	    inline_watch_enabled: bool,
   199	    moe_implementation: MoeImplementation | None,
   200	    remat_mode: RematMode,
   201	    processes_per_task: int = 1,
   202	) -> None:
   203	    env_defaults = dict(HERO_EP_RUNTIME_ENV)
   204	    if processes_per_task > 1:
   205	        # With one process per GPU, the per-process CUPTI sessions collide with each
   206	        # other and with CoreWeave's DCGM, so PGLE cannot profile and its recompile
   207	        # machinery only adds failure modes. Default it off; an explicit env wins.
   208	        env_defaults["JAX_ENABLE_PGLE"] = "false"
   209	    for name, value in env_defaults.items():
   210	        os.environ.setdefault(name, value)
   211	    xla_flags = os.environ.get("XLA_FLAGS", "").split()
   212	    ragged = moe_implementation == RAGGED_MOE_IMPLEMENTATION
   213	    if ragged:
   214	        overlap_limit = RAGGED_COLLECTIVE_OVERLAP_LIMIT
   215	    elif inline_watch_enabled:
   216	        overlap_limit = INLINE_WATCH_COLLECTIVE_OVERLAP_LIMIT
   217	    else:
   218	        overlap_limit = DEFAULT_COLLECTIVE_OVERLAP_LIMIT
   219	    # The scheduler's longer buffer live ranges fit on the ragged transport only once the layer
   220	    # carry leaves HBM. Without that offload its first-step NCCL allocations fail.
   221	    latency_hiding = not ragged or remat_mode == "offload_carry"
   222	    flag_defaults = (
   223	        f"{XLA_COLLECTIVE_OVERLAP_FLAG}={overlap_limit}",
   224	        f"--xla_gpu_enable_latency_hiding_scheduler={'true' if latency_hiding else 'false'}",
   225	        # The scheduler sizes the single `jit_train_step` temp arena against this percentage of
   226	        # its memory budget, roughly `133.6 GiB x percentage`. The pool holds 138.2 GiB and
   227	        # persistent state occupies 18.1 GiB of it, so an arena above 120.2 GiB cannot be served
   228	        # from pool free space and forces a fresh mapping against the ~17 GiB of physical memory
   229	        # outside the pool. The default 95 asks for 125.7 GiB and fails that way. 85 sizes the
   230	        # arena at 113.6 GiB, leaving enough slack for per-node variation in fragmentation. A
   231	        # lower percentage costs throughput, because a smaller arena makes `HloRematerialization`
   232	        # recompute more of the step.
   233	        "--xla_gpu_memory_limit_slop_factor=85",
   234	        XLA_DISABLE_GPU_COMMAND_BUFFER_FLAG,
   235	    )
   236	    explicit_names = {flag.partition("=")[0] for flag in xla_flags}
   237	    xla_flags.extend(flag for flag in flag_defaults if flag.partition("=")[0] not in explicit_names)
   238	    if remat_mode == "offload_carry":
   239	        # Not overridable, unlike the defaults above, including the scheduler flag it pairs with.
   240	        # Both flags can be inherited from another recipe, but only this one fails silently: a
   241	        # wrong overlap limit corrupts training, while an inherited scheduler setting either
   242	        # aborts in NCCL's first-step allocation and names itself, or runs the slower posture.
   243	        xla_flags = [f for f in xla_flags if f.partition("=")[0] != XLA_COLLECTIVE_OVERLAP_FLAG]
   244	        xla_flags.append(f"{XLA_COLLECTIVE_OVERLAP_FLAG}={OFFLOAD_CARRY_COLLECTIVE_OVERLAP_LIMIT}")
   245	    if ragged:
   246	        # Unlike the defaults above, these are not overridable. Selecting the host-launched
   247	        # one-shot kernel needs both flags cleared together plus a splits-per-peer count this
   248	        # branch no longer carries, so honoring a partial override would run a configuration
   249	        # nothing here measures. Drop any conflicting entry rather than relying on which
   250	        # occurrence XLA's parser keeps.
   251	        xla_flags = [f for f in xla_flags if f.partition("=")[0] not in _RAGGED_REQUIRED_XLA_FLAG_NAMES]
   252	        xla_flags.extend(RAGGED_REQUIRED_XLA_FLAGS)
   253	    os.environ["XLA_FLAGS"] = " ".join(xla_flags)
   254

   715	
   716	def _drop_metrics(
   717	    dropped_assignments: jax.Array,
   718	    sender_dropped_assignments: jax.Array,
   719	    receiver_dropped_assignments: jax.Array,
   720	    *,
   721	    batch_size: int,
   722	    sequence_length: int,
   723	    top_k: int,
   724	    num_layers: int,
   725	) -> dict[str, int | float]:
   726	    # Per-layer int32 counts summed over layers in int64 on the host: the global totals exceed int32 at
   727	    # large batch (jax_enable_x64 is off, so an in-device sum would overflow), and float32 would round them.
   728	    def _sum_int64(per_layer: jax.Array) -> int:
   729	        return int(np.asarray(per_layer).astype(np.int64).sum())
   730	
   731	    dropped_assignments_host = _sum_int64(dropped_assignments)
   732	    sender_dropped_assignments_host = _sum_int64(sender_dropped_assignments)
   733	    receiver_dropped_assignments_host = _sum_int64(receiver_dropped_assignments)
   734	    if dropped_assignments_host != sender_dropped_assignments_host + receiver_dropped_assignments_host:
   735	        raise ValueError("total dropped assignments must equal sender plus receiver dropped assignments")
   736	    total_assignments = batch_size * sequence_length * top_k * num_layers
   737	    receiver_assignments = total_assignments - sender_dropped_assignments_host
   738	    return {
   739	        "moe/dropped_assignments": dropped_assignments_host,
   740	        "moe/drop_fraction": dropped_assignments_host / total_assignments,
   741	        "moe/sender_dropped_assignments": sender_dropped_assignments_host,
   742	        "moe/sender_drop_fraction": sender_dropped_assignments_host / total_assignments,
   743	        "moe/receiver_dropped_assignments": receiver_dropped_assignments_host,
   744	        "moe/receiver_drop_fraction": receiver_dropped_assignments_host / total_assignments,
   745	        "moe/receiver_drop_fraction_of_received": receiver_dropped_assignments_host / max(receiver_assignments, 1),
   746	    }

    54	@dataclass
    55	class StepInfo(Generic[S]):
    56	    """
    57	    Information about a step that was just completed. This includes the trainer state, the loss, and the duration of the
    58	    step.
    59	
    60	    Note that the step is 0-indexed, so if you want the next step, use `next_step`.
    61	    """
    62	
    63	    state: S
    64	    loss: float
    65	    step_duration: float
    66	    _event_handler: Callable[[ProgressEvent], None] = field(
    67	        default=_ignore_progress_event,
    68	        repr=False,
    69	        compare=False,
    70	    )
    71	
    72	    model = property(lambda self: self.state.model)
    73	    opt_state = property(lambda self: self.state.opt_state)
    74	    eval_model = property(lambda self: self.state.eval_model)
    75	
    76	    step = property(lambda self: int(self.state.step) - 1)
    77	    """
    78	    The step that was just completed. If you want the next step, use `next_step`.
    79	    """
    80	
    81	    next_step = property(lambda self: int(self.state.step))
    82	
    83	    def emit_event(self, event: ProgressEvent) -> None:
    84	        """Notify event-aware callbacks about progress inside a step callback."""
    85	        self._event_handler(event)
    86	
    87	
    88	class Callback(ABC, Generic[S]):
    89	    """
    90	    A callback that can be called at the end of a step. This is useful for logging, profiling, and other side effects.
    91	    """
    92	
    93	    @abc.abstractmethod
    94	    def on_step(self, info: StepInfo[S], force: bool = False): ...
    95	
    96	    def on_event(self, event: ProgressEvent) -> None:
    97	        del event

   120	def build_hero_configs(*, num_train_steps: int, batch_size: int) -> tuple[GrugModelConfig, GrugMoeMuonHConfig]:
   121	    """The fixed EP64 hero model plus its compute-scaled MuonH optimizer."""
   122	    model = HERO_MODEL
   123	    optimizer = MoeHeuristic().build_optimizer_config(
   124	        num_train_steps=num_train_steps,
   125	        batch_size=batch_size,
   175	    qb_hist_bins: int = 1000  # bins for the QB histogram estimator; ignored by the top-k estimator
   176	    num_layers: int = 6
   177	    num_heads: int = 4
   178	    num_kv_heads: int = 1
   179	    local_kv_heads: int | None = None
   180	    global_kv_heads: int | None = None

   201	    backward skips re-running expert dispatch and its EP collectives; "offload_carry"
   202	    recomputes everything but stages the layer-input residual on pinned host, which

   207	    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
   208	        _ = self.inferred_head_dim
   209	        if self.num_heads % self.stored_kv_heads != 0:
   210	            raise ValueError("num_heads must be divisible by the stored KV-head count")
   211	        if (self.local_kv_heads is None) != (self.global_kv_heads is None):
   212	            raise ValueError("local_kv_heads and global_kv_heads must be set together")
   213	        if self.local_kv_heads is not None and self.global_kv_heads is not None:
   214	            if self.local_kv_heads <= 0 or self.global_kv_heads <= 0:
   215	                raise ValueError("local_kv_heads and global_kv_heads must be positive")
   216	            if self.num_heads % self.local_kv_heads != 0 or self.num_heads % self.global_kv_heads != 0:
   217	                raise ValueError("num_heads must be divisible by both local and global KV-head counts")
   218	            if self.num_kv_heads != max(self.local_kv_heads, self.global_kv_heads):
   219	                raise ValueError("num_kv_heads must equal the stored maximum of local/global KV heads")
   220	        if self.global_every <= 0:
   221	            raise ValueError("global_every must be positive")
   222	        if self.vocab_size <= 0:
   223	            raise ValueError("vocab_size must be positive")
   224	        if self.max_seq_len <= 0:
   225	            raise ValueError("max_seq_len must be positive")
   226	        if self.num_experts <= 0:
   227	            raise ValueError("num_experts must be positive")
   228	        if self.num_experts_per_token <= 0:
   229	            raise ValueError("num_experts_per_token must be positive")
   230	        if self.num_experts_per_token >= self.num_experts:
   231	            # QB routing takes top-(k+1) and keeps the last entry as the threshold alpha, so a
   232	            # full-bank top-k asks `jax.lax.top_k` for more entries than the router has experts.
   233	            raise ValueError("num_experts_per_token must be < num_experts, because QB routing selects top-(k+1)")
   234	        if self.latent_dim is not None and not 0 < self.latent_dim <= self.hidden_dim:
   235	            raise ValueError(
   236	                f"latent_dim must be in (0, hidden_dim={self.hidden_dim}], got {self.latent_dim}; "
   237	                "it only reduces communication when it is smaller than the hidden dimension"
   238	            )
   239	        if self.shared_expert_intermediate_dim < 0:
   240	            raise ValueError("shared_expert_intermediate_dim must be non-negative")
   241	        if self.capacity_factor <= 0:
   242	            raise ValueError("capacity_factor must be positive")
   243	        if self.expert_chunks <= 0:
   244	            raise ValueError("expert_chunks must be positive")
   245	        if self.pooled_transport_capacity_factor is not None and self.pooled_transport_capacity_factor <= 0:
   246	            raise ValueError("pooled_transport_capacity_factor must be positive")
   247	        if self.moe_implementation == "fixed_pooled_wave_all_to_all":
   248	            if self.pooled_transport_capacity_factor is None:
   249	                raise ValueError("fixed_pooled_wave_all_to_all requires pooled_transport_capacity_factor")
   250	        if self.num_expert_waves <= 0:
   251	            raise ValueError("num_expert_waves must be positive")
   252	        if self.num_shared_experts <= 0:
   253	            raise ValueError("num_shared_experts must be positive")
   254	        resolve_moe_implementation(self.moe_implementation)
   255

   257	    def Embed(self) -> Axis:
   258	        return Axis("embed", self.hidden_dim)

   871	    def _run_checkpoint_loop(self, stop_event: threading.Event) -> None:
   872	        """Periodic checkpoint loop: runs on its own thread so the multi-second
   873	        backup+upload doesn't stall the control tick cadence."""
   874	        limiter = self._periodic_checkpoint_limiter
   875	        assert limiter is not None, "checkpoint loop spawned without configured limiter"
   876	        while not stop_event.is_set():
   877	            if not limiter.wait(cancel=stop_event):
   878	                break
   879	            try:
   880	                write_checkpoint(self._db, self._config.remote_state_dir)
   881	            except Exception:
   882	                logger.exception("Periodic checkpoint failed")

   884	    # =========================================================================
   885	    # Unified control tick
   886	    # =========================================================================

   888	    def _run_control_loop(self, stop_event: threading.Event) -> None:
   889	        """Single driver: schedule -> reconcile -> autoscale as phases of one tick.
   890	
   891	        Each iteration builds one read snapshot, runs the phases that are due (or,
   892	        on a wake, a schedule-only mini-tick), folds backend-observed health, and
   893	        commits through a single end-of-tick write transaction. Wakes every
   894	        ``poll_interval`` (the reconcile cadence) or sooner on a submit/wake, so
   895	        the per-phase cadences match the legacy three-loop structure.
   896	        """
   897	        base_interval = self._config.poll_interval.to_seconds()
   898	        schedule_limiter = RateLimiter(interval_seconds=self._config.scheduler_min_interval.to_seconds())
   899	        reconcile_limiter = RateLimiter(interval_seconds=self._config.poll_interval.to_seconds())
   900	        autoscale_limiter = RateLimiter(interval_seconds=self._config.autoscaler_evaluation_interval.to_seconds())
   901	        while not stop_event.is_set():
   902	            woken = self._tick_wake.wait(timeout=base_interval)
   903	            self._tick_wake.clear()
   904	            if stop_event.is_set():
   905	                break
   906	            try:
   907	                self._control_tick(
   908	                    woken=woken,
   909	                    schedule_limiter=schedule_limiter,
   910	                    reconcile_limiter=reconcile_limiter,

     1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2	# Submit one hero EP64 arm for the 8753-mfu loop (research/mcwitt/8753-mfu-loop, #8753 head base).
     3	#
     4	# Protocol (fixed for every iteration so arms stay comparable; see DESIGN.md):
     5	#   - one NVL72 rack on cw-us-east-08a, PRODUCTION priority allowed with job runtime <1h
     6	#     (user 2026-08-28): ARM_TIMEOUT 3500s outer backstop, watchdog.sh COMPILE_CEILING=1800 +
     7	#     STEP_BUDGET=1200 inner caps (20-min post-compile cap)
     8	#   - restore from the live hero's step-30000 checkpoint, mixture data (trained-router routing);
     9	#     NUM_STEPS is the ABSOLUTE stop step (checkpoint step + window), never a relative count
    10	#   - no checkpoints, no eval; profiler off on SCORED arms (a profile tail on an otherwise-scored
    11	#     draw is allowed: PROFILE_STEPS>0 with PROFILE_START_STEP as an ABSOLUTE step past the
    12	#     scoring window, e.g. 30021)
    13	#   - no MARIN_PJRT_WHEEL overlay: the branch's uv.lock pins jax_cuda13_pjrt 0.11.1+marin.c9526e8c0272
    14	#   - JAX_COMPILATION_CACHE_DIR rotated per run id (clique-init deadlock dodge); a RESUBMITTED
    15	#     arm must use a fresh RID (and VERSION): reusing one merges W&B histories, reuses the
    16	#     leader-populated compile cache (clique-deadlock recipe), and can vacuously reuse artifacts
    17	#
    18	# usage: RID=<run-id> VERSION=<calver> [REPO=<worktree>] [EXTRA_LAUNCH_ARGS="..."] arm.sh
    19	set -euo pipefail
    20	
    21	: "${WANDB_API_KEY:?set WANDB_API_KEY}"
    22	RID="${RID:?set RID}"
    23	VERSION="${VERSION:?set VERSION -- bump it per arm or the artifact layer reuses the last run}"
    24	
    25	LOOP_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
    26	REPO="${REPO:-$(cd "${LOOP_DIR}/../.." && pwd)}"
    27	
    28	# Iris bundles the working tree, not HEAD: uncommitted edits (including another session writing
    29	# to a shared checkout) would ship silently and break arm attribution. The loop dir itself is
    30	# exempt -- it accumulates logs between commits.
    31	if [ -z "${ALLOW_DIRTY:-}" ] && git -C "$REPO" status --porcelain | grep -v "autoresearch/" | grep -q .; then
    32	  echo "refusing to submit from a dirty tree (set ALLOW_DIRTY=1 to override):" >&2
    33	  git -C "$REPO" status --porcelain | grep -v "autoresearch/" >&2
    34	  exit 1
    35	fi
    36	
    37	PRIORITY="${PRIORITY:-production}"
    38	ARM_TIMEOUT="${ARM_TIMEOUT:-3500}"
    39	NUM_STEPS="${NUM_STEPS:-30030}"
    40	TRAINING_DATA="${TRAINING_DATA:-mixture}"
    41	MASTER_PARAMS="${MASTER_PARAMS:-device}"
    42	MOE_IMPL="${MOE_IMPL:-ragged_all_to_all}"
    43	RESTORE_FROM="${RESTORE_FROM:-s3://marin-us-east-02a/marin/grug/hero-12d8b6f0-dee637/2026.08.19.2/checkpoints/step-30000}"
    44	RESTORE_ARGS=()
    45	if [ -n "$RESTORE_FROM" ]; then
    46	  RESTORE_ARGS=(--restore-from "$RESTORE_FROM")
    47	fi
    48	# The learning-rate schedule's length; fixed across arms so losses stay comparable.
    49	SCHEDULE_STEPS=4470000
    50	
    51	read -r -a EXTRA <<<"${EXTRA_LAUNCH_ARGS:-}"
    52	
    53	cd "$REPO"
    54	uv run iris --config lib/iris/config/marin.yaml job run \
    55	  --no-wait --enable-extra-resources \
    56	  --target-cluster cw-us-east-08a --priority "${PRIORITY}" \
    57	  --cpu 2 --memory 8GB --disk 32GB --timeout "${ARM_TIMEOUT}" \
    58	  --job-name "${RID}-coord" \
    59	  -e IRIS_USER mwittmann \
    60	  -e WANDB_API_KEY "${WANDB_API_KEY}" \
    61	  -e WANDB_PROJECT marin_moe \
    62	  -e MARIN_PREFIX s3://marin-us-east-02a/marin \
    63	  -e IRIS_PORT_JAX 32703 \
    64	  -e AWS_MAX_ATTEMPTS 25 -e AWS_RETRY_MODE adaptive \
    65	  -e JAX_COMPILATION_CACHE_DIR "s3://marin-us-east-02a/marin/tmp/ttl=30d/jaxcache/${RID}" \
    66	  -e XLA_FLAGS "${ARM_XLA_FLAGS:-}" \
    67	  -- python -m experiments.grug.moe_hero_ep.launch_diagnostics \
    68	     --run-id "${RID}" \
    69	     --dp-racks 1 --num-steps "${NUM_STEPS}" --schedule-steps "${SCHEDULE_STEPS}" \
    70	     --capacity-factor 1.15 \
    71	     --moe-implementation "${MOE_IMPL}" \
    72	     --processes-per-task 4 \
    73	     --master-params "${MASTER_PARAMS}" \
    74	     --training-data "${TRAINING_DATA}" \
    75	     --profile-steps "${PROFILE_STEPS:-0}" --profile-start-step "${PROFILE_START_STEP:-30021}" \
    76	     --watch-interval 0 --eval-every 0 \
    77	     --no-save-checkpoints \
    78	     "${RESTORE_ARGS[@]}" \
    79	     "${EXTRA[@]}" \
    80	     --version "${VERSION}" --run >"${LOOP_DIR}/${RID}-submit.log" 2>&1 \
    81	  || { echo "submit FAILED, tail of ${RID}-submit.log:" >&2; tail -20 "${LOOP_DIR}/${RID}-submit.log" >&2; exit 1; }
    82	
    83	printf '%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\n' \
    84	  "${RID}" "$(git -C "$REPO" rev-parse --short HEAD)" "${VERSION}" "${MOE_IMPL}" "${TRAINING_DATA}" \
    85	  "${MASTER_PARAMS}" "${SCHEDULE_STEPS}" "${ARM_TIMEOUT}" "${RESTORE_FROM:-none}" \
    86	  "${EXTRA_LAUNCH_ARGS:-none}" >> "${LOOP_DIR}/arms.tsv"
    87	
    88	echo "submitted ${RID} at ${PRIORITY} priority (timeout ${ARM_TIMEOUT}s, commit $(git -C "$REPO" rev-parse --short HEAD), data ${TRAINING_DATA}, extra: ${EXTRA_LAUNCH_ARGS:-none})"
    55	    _compute_flops,
    56	    run_grug,
    57	)
    58	
    59	DEFAULT_HERO_STEPS = 25
    60	HERO_CHECKPOINT_INTERVAL = timedelta(minutes=15)
    61	
    62	
    63	def build_diagnostic_run(
    64	    *,
    65	    run_id: str,
    66	    restore_from: str | None = None,
    67	    dp_racks: int,
    68	    num_steps: int,
    69	    schedule_steps: int | None = None,
    70	    seed: int = 0,
    71	    batch_size: int = HERO_EP_BATCH_SIZE,
    72	    num_experts: int | None = None,
    73	    num_experts_per_token: int | None = None,
    74	    intermediate_dim: int | None = None,
    75	    capacity_factor: float | None = None,
    76	    latent_dim: int | None = None,
    77	    moe_implementation: str | None = None,
    78	    master_param_mode: MasterParamMode = HERO_MASTER_PARAM_MODE,
    79	    processes_per_task: int = HERO_PROCESSES_PER_TASK,
    80	    eval_every: int = 0,
    81	    save_checkpoints: bool = False,
    82	    checkpoint_interval: timedelta = HERO_CHECKPOINT_INTERVAL,
    83	    checkpoint_path: str | None = None,
    84	    checkpoint_debug: CheckpointDebugConfig | None = None,
    85	    watch_interval: int = HERO_WATCH_INTERVAL,
    86	    watch_mode: WatchMode = WatchMode.INLINE,
    87	    profile_steps: int = 0,
    88	    profile_start_step: int = 5,
    89	    training_data_mode: TrainingDataMode = TrainingDataMode.MIXTURE,
    90	    version: str | None = None,
    91	) -> ArtifactStep[HeroThroughputResult]:
    92	    """Build a bounded diagnostic run for the production EP64 hero recipe.
    93	
    94	    The overrides sweep expert count, expert width, routed top-k, and routing capacity from the
    95	    hero spec. They keep the hidden dimension, so the compute-scaled optimizer values stay
    96	    comparable across a sweep. ``None`` keeps the hero value.
    97	
    98	    ``batch_size`` is the global batch across all data-parallel racks. It does not scale with
    99	    ``dp_racks``. ``batch_size`` and ``schedule_steps`` change the token budget for the heuristic.
   100	    """
   101	    if not run_id.strip():
   102	        raise ValueError("run_id must not be empty")
   103	    if dp_racks <= 0:
   104	        raise ValueError(f"dp_racks must be positive, got {dp_racks}")
   105	    if num_steps <= 0:
   106	        raise ValueError(f"num_steps must be positive, got {num_steps}")
   107	    if checkpoint_interval <= timedelta(0):
   108	        raise ValueError(f"checkpoint_interval must be positive, got {checkpoint_interval}")
   109	    if profile_steps < 0:
   110	        raise ValueError(f"profile_steps must be non-negative, got {profile_steps}")
   111	    if profile_start_step < 0:
   112	        raise ValueError(f"profile_start_step must be non-negative, got {profile_start_step}")
   113	    if profile_steps > 0 and profile_start_step >= num_steps:
   114	        raise ValueError(f"profile_start_step must be less than num_steps={num_steps}, got {profile_start_step}")
   115	    # `schedule_steps` sets the whole learning-rate schedule; `num_steps` is the absolute step the
   116	    # run stops at (a restore resumes mid-schedule, so it must lie past the restored step).
   117	    # Both matter, and they enter in different places. The optimizer heuristic scales learning rate,
   118	    # adam_lr, and epsilon from a token budget (`num_train_steps * batch * seq`), which fixes the
   119	    # peak. Warmup and decay are *fractions* of `TrainerConfig.num_train_steps`, so that field has to
   120	    # carry the schedule length too -- passing `num_steps` there warms up in `0.01 * num_steps` and
   121	    # decays to `min_lr_ratio` by the end of the short run, which is a whole miniature schedule
   122	    # rather than the head of a long one. Default keeps the two equal, which is the previous behavior.
   123	    if schedule_steps is not None and schedule_steps <= 0:
   124	        raise ValueError(f"schedule_steps must be positive, got {schedule_steps}")
   125	    if schedule_steps is not None and schedule_steps < num_steps:
   126	        raise ValueError(f"schedule_steps={schedule_steps} must be at least num_steps={num_steps}")
   127	    total_schedule_steps = schedule_steps if schedule_steps is not None else num_steps
   128	    _, optimizer = build_hero_configs(
   129	        num_train_steps=total_schedule_steps,
   130	        batch_size=batch_size,
   131	    )
   132	    model = HERO_MODEL_CONFIG
   133	    overrides = {
   134	        name: value
   135	        for name, value in (
   136	            ("num_experts", num_experts),
   137	            ("num_experts_per_token", num_experts_per_token),
   138	            ("intermediate_dim", intermediate_dim),
   139	            ("capacity_factor", capacity_factor),
   140	            ("latent_dim", latent_dim),
   141	            ("moe_implementation", moe_implementation),
   142	        )
   143	        if value is not None
   144	    }
   145	    if overrides:
   146	        model = dataclasses.replace(model, **overrides)
   147	    model = with_transport_remat_mode(model)
   148	    # A bank that is not divisible by the expert axis fails inside `moe_mlp`, which is after the rack
   149	    # is already allocated and the workspace is built. Reject it here instead.
   150	    if model.num_experts % HERO_EP_EXPERT_AXIS_SIZE != 0:
   151	        raise ValueError(f"num_experts={model.num_experts} must be divisible by {HERO_EP_EXPERT_AXIS_SIZE}")
   152	    local_experts = model.num_experts // HERO_EP_EXPERT_AXIS_SIZE
   153	    if local_experts % model.num_expert_waves != 0:
   154	        raise ValueError(
   155	            f"local expert count={local_experts} must be divisible by num_expert_waves={model.num_expert_waves}"
   156	        )
   157	    # The ragged transport requires one GPU per process; fail fast if this is not satisfied.
   158	    if model.moe_implementation == RAGGED_MOE_IMPLEMENTATION and processes_per_task != HERO_GPUS_PER_NODE:
   159	        raise ValueError(
   160	            f"{RAGGED_MOE_IMPLEMENTATION} needs one process per GPU: pass "
   161	            f"processes_per_task={HERO_GPUS_PER_NODE}, got {processes_per_task}"
   162	        )
   163	    pooled = model.moe_implementation == "fixed_pooled_wave_all_to_all"
   164	    if pooled and model.pooled_transport_capacity_factor is None:
   165	        raise AssertionError("the pooled-wave hero requires a transport capacity factor")
   166	    backend_tag = model.moe_implementation.replace("_", "-")
   167	    capacity_tag = f"capacity-{model.capacity_factor:g}"
   168	    # Only the pooled transport has a receiver capacity of its own to report.
   169	    transport_capacity_tags = (f"transport-capacity-{model.pooled_transport_capacity_factor:g}",) if pooled else ()
   170	    wave_tag = f"expert-waves-{model.num_expert_waves}"
   171	    size_tag = f"e{model.num_experts}-i{model.intermediate_dim}"
   172	    wandb_project = os.environ.get("WANDB_PROJECT") or DEFAULT_WANDB_PROJECT
   173	    grug_trainer = hero_grug_trainer_config(
   174	        replica_axis_size=dp_racks,
   175	        training_data_mode=training_data_mode,
   176	        watch_mode=watch_mode,
   177	        save_checkpoints=save_checkpoints,
   178	        master_param_mode=master_param_mode,
   179	    )
   180	    train_resources = ResourceConfig.with_gpu(
   181	        "GB200",
   182	        count=HERO_GPUS_PER_NODE,
   183	        cpu=HERO_NODE_CPU,
   184	        ram=HERO_NODE_RAM,
   185	        disk=HERO_NODE_DISK,
   186	        replicas=HERO_EP_NODES * dp_racks,
   187	    )
   188	    name = f"grug/{run_id}"
   189	    version = resolve_version(name, version)
   190	    validation = validation_datasets() if eval_every > 0 else []
   191	    flops_per_example, _ = _compute_flops(model_config=model)
   192	    experiment_flops = flops_per_example * batch_size * total_schedule_steps
   193	
   194	    def build_config(ctx: StepContext) -> GrugRunConfig:
   195	        trainer = hero_trainer_config(
   196	            run_id=run_id,
   197	            seed=seed,
   198	            train_batch_size=batch_size,
   199	            num_train_steps=total_schedule_steps,
   200	            master_param_mode=master_param_mode,
   201	            load_checkpoint_path=restore_from,
   202	            profiler=ProfilerConfig(
   203	                enabled=profile_steps > 0,
   204	                start_step=profile_start_step,
   205	                num_steps=profile_steps,
   206	                # One rank is enough for a step trace, and tracing all 64 multiplies the upload
   207	                # without adding signal.
   208	                process_index=0,
   209	                # Host scopes and HLO metadata identify compiled model regions in XProf.
   210	                profile_options=ProfileOptionsConfig(
   211	                    host_tracer_level=1,
   212	                    python_tracer_level=0,
   213	                    enable_hlo_proto=True,
   214	                ),
   215	            ),
   216	            tracker=WandbConfig(
   217	                entity="marin-community",
   218	                project=wandb_project,
   219	                tags=[
   220	                    "grug",
   221	                    "moe",
   222	                    "hero",
   223	                    "ep",
   224	                    backend_tag,
   225	                    capacity_tag,
   226	                    f"master-params-{master_param_mode.value.replace('_', '-')}",
   227	                    *transport_capacity_tags,
   228	                    wave_tag,
   229	                    size_tag,
   230	                    "gb200",
   231	                    HARRIER_MIX_2026_08_18_TAG,
   232	                    "MHEP",
   233	                ],
   234	                group="moe-hero-ep",
   235	                name=run_id,
   236	                replicate_path=ctx.output_path,
   237	            ),
   238	            watch=WatchConfig(interval=watch_interval),
   239	            # Levanter's default base path is pod-local, so a preempted run would have nothing to
   240	            # resume from. `checkpoint_path` overrides this for runs targeting disposable storage.
   241	            checkpointer=CheckpointerConfig(
   242	                base_path=checkpoint_path or prefix_join(ctx.output_path, "checkpoints"),
   243	                temporary_base_path=None,
   244	                save_interval=checkpoint_interval,
   245	                keep=None,
   246	                append_run_id_to_base_path=False,
   247	                delete_old_temp_checkpoints=True,
   248	                keep_last_temporary_checkpoints=1,
   249	                debug=checkpoint_debug or CheckpointDebugConfig(),
   250	            ),
   251	        )
   252	        data = harrier_mix_2026_08_18_data_config(
   253	            ctx=ctx,
   254	            total_steps=total_schedule_steps,
   255	            batch_size=batch_size,
   256	            max_seq_len=model.max_seq_len,
   257	            experiment_flops=experiment_flops,
   258	            validation=validation,
   259	        )
   260	        return GrugRunConfig(
   285	        name=user_namespaced_name(name, version),
   286	        version=version,
   287	        artifact_type=HeroThroughputResult,
   288	        run=run_grug,
   289	        build_config=build_config,
   290	        deps=(HARRIER_MIX_2026_08_18_STORE, *validation),
   291	        runtime_args={"train_resources": train_resources},
   292	    )
   293	
   294	
   295	@click.command()
   296	@click.option("--run-id", required=True, help="Run identifier for artifact and W&B names.")
   297	@click.option(
   298	    "--dp-racks",
   299	    type=click.IntRange(min=1),
   300	    default=1,
   301	    show_default=True,
   302	    help="Data-parallel NVL72 rack count. --batch-size stays global across all racks.",
   303	)
   304	@click.option(
   305	    "--num-steps",
   306	    type=click.IntRange(min=1),
   307	    default=DEFAULT_HERO_STEPS,
   308	    show_default=True,
   309	    help=(
   310	        "Number of steps at which training terminates. When restoring from a checkpoint, "
   311	        "the number of steps taken in this run will be less than --num-steps "
   312	        "by the number of steps at the checkpoint."
   313	    ),
   314	)
   315	@click.option(
   316	    "--schedule-steps",
   317	    type=click.IntRange(min=1),
   318	    default=None,
   319	    help=(
   320	        "Build the learning-rate schedule for this many steps instead of --num-steps. The optimizer "
   321	        "heuristic scales its rates from the implied token budget, so this trains the head of a long "
   322	        "run's schedule. Defaults to --num-steps."
   323	    ),
   324	)
   325	@click.option(
   326	    "--seed",
   327	    type=int,
   328	    default=0,
   329	    help="Trainer seed. Vary it across otherwise identical runs to measure run-to-run variance.",
   330	)
   331	@click.option(
   332	    "--num-experts",
   333	    type=click.IntRange(min=1),
   334	    default=HERO_MODEL_CONFIG.num_experts,
   335	    show_default=True,
   336	    help=(
   337	        f"Override the routed expert count. The count must be divisible by {HERO_EP_EXPERT_AXIS_SIZE}, "
   338	        f"and the local expert count must support {HERO_MODEL_CONFIG.num_expert_waves} waves."
   339	    ),
   340	)
   341	@click.option(
   342	    "--num-experts-per-token",
   343	    type=click.IntRange(min=1),
   344	    default=HERO_MODEL_CONFIG.num_experts_per_token,
   345	    show_default=True,
   346	    help="Override the routed top-k. Scales both active parameters and the EP dispatch buffers.",
   347	)
   348	@click.option(
   349	    "--intermediate-dim",
   350	    type=click.IntRange(min=1),
   351	    default=HERO_MODEL_CONFIG.intermediate_dim,
   352	    show_default=True,
   353	    help="Override the routed expert width.",
   354	)
   355	@click.option(
   356	    "--batch-size",
   357	    type=click.IntRange(min=1),
   358	    default=HERO_EP_BATCH_SIZE,
   359	    show_default=True,
   360	    help="Global sequences per step. This value does not scale with --dp-racks.",
   361	)
   362	@click.option(
   363	    "--moe-implementation",
   364	    default=None,
   365	    help="Override the MoE backend, e.g. ragged_all_to_all. Defaults to the hero spec.",
   366	)
   367	@click.option(
   368	    "--master-params",
   369	    type=click.Choice([mode.value for mode in MasterParamMode]),
   370	    default=HERO_MASTER_PARAM_MODE.value,
   371	    show_default=True,
   372	    help=("Where the authoritative fp32 weights live: on device, or as a pinned-host master."),
   373	)
   374	@click.option(
   375	    "--processes-per-task",
   376	    type=click.IntRange(min=1),
   377	    default=HERO_PROCESSES_PER_TASK,
   378	    show_default=True,
   379	    help="JAX processes per node.",
   380	)
   381	@click.option(
   382	    "--latent-dim",
   383	    type=click.IntRange(min=1),
   384	    default=None,
   385	    help="LatentMoE: run routed experts at this width. Divides all-to-all traffic by hidden/latent.",
   386	)
   387	@click.option(
   388	    "--save-checkpoints/--no-save-checkpoints",
   389	    default=False,
   390	    show_default=True,
   391	    help="Write periodic and final checkpoints, and resume from the newest complete checkpoint.",
   392	)
   393	@click.option(
   394	    "--checkpoint-minutes",
   395	    type=click.FloatRange(min=0, min_open=True),
   396	    default=HERO_CHECKPOINT_INTERVAL.total_seconds() / 60,
   397	    show_default=True,
   398	    help="Wall-clock minutes between checkpoint writes.",
   399	)
   400	@click.option(
   401	    "--checkpoint-path",
   402	    default=None,
   403	    help="Checkpoint output path, e.g. a marin_temp_bucket() path. Defaults to the step output path.",
   404	)
   405	@click.option(
   406	    "--checkpoint-debug/--no-checkpoint-debug",
   407	    default=False,
   408	    show_default=True,
   409	    help="Publish checkpoint phase and memory telemetry. Use with --save-checkpoints.",
   410	)
   411	@click.option(
   412	    "--eval-every",
   413	    type=click.IntRange(min=0),
   414	    default=0,
   415	    show_default=True,
   416	    help="Run the paloma suite every N steps. 0 disables eval (throughput-only run).",
   417	)
   418	@click.option(
   419	    "--watch-interval",
   420	    type=click.IntRange(min=0),
   421	    default=HERO_WATCH_INTERVAL,
   422	    show_default=True,
   423	    help="Steps between gradient and parameter norm logs. 0 disables norm logging.",
   424	)
   425	@click.option(
   426	    "--watch-mode",
   427	    type=click.Choice([mode.value for mode in WatchMode]),
   428	    default=WatchMode.INLINE.value,
   429	    show_default=True,
   430	    help="Compute norms in the training step or in a separate forward and backward diagnostic step.",
   431	)
   432	@click.option(
   433	    "--profile-steps",
   434	    type=click.IntRange(min=0),
   435	    default=0,
   436	    show_default=True,
   437	    help="Steps to trace with XProf on rank 0. 0 disables the profiler.",
   438	)
   439	@click.option(
   440	    "--profile-start-step",
   441	    type=click.IntRange(min=0),
   442	    default=5,
   443	    show_default=True,
   444	    help="First traced step. Keep it past compile and warmup.",
   445	)
   446	@click.option(
   447	    "--training-data",
   448	    type=click.Choice([mode.value for mode in TrainingDataMode]),
   449	    default=TrainingDataMode.MIXTURE.value,
   450	    show_default=True,
   451	    help="Use the configured mixture or reuse a deterministic synthetic batch without opening TensorStore.",
   452	)
   453	@click.option(
   454	    "--capacity-factor",
   455	    type=click.FloatRange(min=0, min_open=True),
   456	    default=HERO_MODEL_CONFIG.capacity_factor,
   457	    show_default=True,
   458	    help="Override the pooled receiver capacity factor.",
   459	)
   460	@click.option(
   461	    "--restore-from",
   462	    default=None,
   463	    help="Initialize from another run's checkpoint directory, read-only.",
   464	)
   465	@build_options
   466	def main(
   467	    run_id: str,
   468	    restore_from: str | None,
   469	    dp_racks: int,
   470	    num_steps: int,
   471	    schedule_steps: int | None,
   472	    seed: int,
   473	    batch_size: int,
   474	    num_experts: int | None,
   475	    num_experts_per_token: int | None,
   476	    intermediate_dim: int | None,
   477	    capacity_factor: float | None,
   478	    latent_dim: int | None,
   479	    moe_implementation: str | None,
   480	    master_params: str,
   481	    processes_per_task: int,
   482	    save_checkpoints: bool,
   483	    checkpoint_minutes: float,
   484	    checkpoint_path: str | None,
   485	    checkpoint_debug: bool,
   486	    eval_every: int,
   487	    watch_interval: int,
   488	    watch_mode: str,
   489	    profile_steps: int,
   490	    profile_start_step: int,
   491	    training_data: str,
   492	) -> ArtifactStep[HeroThroughputResult]:
   493	    return build_diagnostic_run(
   494	        run_id=run_id,
   495	        restore_from=restore_from,
   496	        dp_racks=dp_racks,
   497	        num_steps=num_steps,
   498	        schedule_steps=schedule_steps,
   499	        seed=seed,
   500	        batch_size=batch_size,
   501	        num_experts=num_experts,
   502	        num_experts_per_token=num_experts_per_token,
   503	        intermediate_dim=intermediate_dim,
   504	        capacity_factor=capacity_factor,
   505	        latent_dim=latent_dim,
   506	        moe_implementation=moe_implementation,
   507	        master_param_mode=MasterParamMode(master_params),
   508	        processes_per_task=processes_per_task,
   509	        save_checkpoints=save_checkpoints,
   510	        checkpoint_interval=timedelta(minutes=checkpoint_minutes),
   511	        checkpoint_path=checkpoint_path,
   512	        checkpoint_debug=(
   513	            CheckpointDebugConfig(
   514	                enabled=True,
   515	                tracemalloc_frames=None,
   516	                top_allocations=0,
   517	                force_gc_before_serialize=False,
   518	                flush_logs=False,
   519	            )
   520	            if checkpoint_debug
   521	            else None
   522	        ),
   523	        eval_every=eval_every,
   524	        watch_interval=watch_interval,
   525	        watch_mode=WatchMode(watch_mode),
   526	        profile_steps=profile_steps,
   527	        profile_start_step=profile_start_step,
   528	        training_data_mode=TrainingDataMode(training_data),
   529	    )
   530	
   531	
   532	if __name__ == "__main__":
   533	    main()

/etc/profiles/per-user/marin/bin/bash -lc 'nl -ba autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/watchdog.sh' in /home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2	# Out-of-process runtime cap, early release, and anomaly alerting for one arm.
     3	#
     4	# Exits (each prints one WATCHDOG marker line; the harness alerts on process exit, so exiting
     5	# fast IS the alert):
     6	#   1. Step budget: cancel STEP_BUDGET seconds after the first step this watch observes. Compile
     7	#      time varies run to run; an arm that pays a slow compile still gets its full measurement
     8	#      window, but an arm whose change makes *steps* slow gets no extra rack time.
     9	#   2. Compile ceiling: cancel if no step has appeared COMPILE_CEILING seconds after submission.
    10	#   3. Early release: cancel as soon as W&B shows the last step the scoring window needs.
    11	#   4. Preemption or task failure: `job describe` publishes failures= and preemptions= counters.
    12	#      Either going nonzero invalidates the measurement (a rescheduled gang recompiles and the
    13	#      step timings straddle the gap), so cancel immediately instead of letting the retry burn
    14	#      the ceiling.
    15	#   5. Coordinator death before the train job exists (e.g. a config guard raising at dispatch):
    16	#      previously this burned the whole compile ceiling; now it exits within one poll.
    17	# Iris's own --timeout on the coord job is the outer backstop; it must cover
    18	# COMPILE_CEILING + STEP_BUDGET. This watchdog runs in a separate process so a wedged CLI or a
    19	# lost session cannot defeat it.
    20	#
    21	# State polls are cheap (one describe RPC), so they run every POLL=20s; the W&B step query is
    22	# heavier and runs every third poll.
    23	#
    24	# usage: RID=<run-id> [STEP_BUDGET=900] [COMPILE_CEILING=1200] [SCORE_MAX_STEP=19] watchdog.sh
    25	set -uo pipefail
    26	
    27	RID="${RID:?set RID}"
    28	STEP_BUDGET="${STEP_BUDGET:-1200}"
    29	COMPILE_CEILING="${COMPILE_CEILING:-1800}"
    30	SCORE_MAX_STEP="${SCORE_MAX_STEP:-19}"
    31	POLL="${POLL:-20}"
    32	STEP_POLL_EVERY="${STEP_POLL_EVERY:-3}"
    33	
    34	LOOP_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
    35	REPO="$(cd "${LOOP_DIR}/../.." && pwd)"
    36	IRIS=(uv run iris --config lib/iris/config/marin.yaml)
    37	COORD="/mwittmann/${RID}-coord"
    38	TRAIN="${COORD}/grug-train-${RID}"
    39	start=$(date +%s)
    40	# A production submission may queue behind another job, so the compile ceiling starts when the
    41	# train job is first observed running, not at submission. QUEUE_CEILING bounds the pending phase.
    42	QUEUE_CEILING="${QUEUE_CEILING:-10800}"
    43	deadline=$(( start + QUEUE_CEILING ))
    44	run_seen=-1
    45	first_step=-1
    46	steps=-1
    47	tick=0
    48	
    49	cancel_arm() {  # cancel_arm <reason>
    50	  echo "$(date -u +%H:%M:%S) cancelling ${COORD}: $1"
    51	  # Cancel the train job by its own path as well as through the coordinator. Iris's --timeout may
    52	  # already have taken the coordinator down, and a cancel routed only through a dead parent is
    53	  # exactly how a 16-node child gets orphaned on the rack.
    54	  ( cd "$REPO" && timeout 300 "${IRIS[@]}" job cancel "$TRAIN" 2>&1 | tail -2 )
    55	  ( cd "$REPO" && timeout 300 "${IRIS[@]}" job cancel "$COORD" 2>&1 | tail -2 )
    56	}
    57	
    58	state_line_of() {  # state_line_of <job path> -> "state failures preemptions" (empty if absent)
    59	  ( cd "$REPO" && timeout 120 "${IRIS[@]}" job describe "$1" 2>/dev/null \
    60	      | sed -n 's/^State: \([a-z_]*\).*failures=\([0-9]*\).*preemptions=\([0-9]*\).*/\1 \2 \3/p' | head -1 )
    61	}
    62	
    63	max_step() {
    64	  ( cd "$REPO" && timeout 180 uv run --with wandb python - "$RID" <<'PY' 2>/dev/null
    65	import sys, wandb
    66	try:
    67	    run = wandb.Api().run(f"marin-community/marin_moe/{sys.argv[1]}")
    68	except Exception:
    69	    print(-2); raise SystemExit
    70	steps = [row.get("_step", -1) for row in run.scan_history(keys=["_step", "throughput/mfu"])]
    71	print(max(steps) if steps else -1)
    72	PY
    73	  )
    74	}
    75	
    76	while :; do
    77	  now=$(date +%s)
    78	  if [ "$now" -ge "$deadline" ]; then
    79	    if [ "${first_step:--1}" -ge 0 ] 2>/dev/null; then
    80	      cancel_arm "step budget ${STEP_BUDGET}s past first step reached"
    81	      echo "WATCHDOG hard-cap step-budget"
    82	    else
    83	      if [ "${run_seen:--1}" -ge 0 ]; then
    84	        cancel_arm "no step within the ${COMPILE_CEILING}s compile ceiling"
    85	        echo "WATCHDOG hard-cap compile-ceiling"
    86	      else
    87	        cancel_arm "still queued after ${QUEUE_CEILING}s"
    88	        echo "WATCHDOG hard-cap queue-ceiling"
    89	      fi
    90	    fi
    91	    exit 0
    92	  fi
    93	
    94	  read -r train_state train_failures train_preemptions <<<"$(state_line_of "$TRAIN")" || true
    95	  if [ -z "${train_state:-}" ]; then
    96	    # No train job yet: the coordinator is either still dispatching or died before dispatch.
    97	    read -r coord_state coord_failures coord_preemptions <<<"$(state_line_of "$COORD")" || true
    98	    case "${coord_state:-}" in
    99	      succeeded|killed|cancelled|*failed)
   100	        cancel_arm "coordinator ${coord_state} before the train job existed"
   101	        echo "WATCHDOG coord-terminal state=${coord_state} failures=${coord_failures:-?}"
   102	        exit 0;;
   103	    esac
   104	  fi
   105	  if [ "${run_seen:--1}" -lt 0 ] && [ "${train_state:-}" = "running" ]; then
   106	    # Iris can report a gang "running" while another job still holds the rack; the W&B run is
   107	    # only created once distributed init actually completes, so gate the compile clock on it.
   108	    probe="$(max_step)"
   109	    if [ "${probe:--2}" -ge -1 ] 2>/dev/null; then
   110	      run_seen=$now
   111	      deadline=$(( now + COMPILE_CEILING ))
   112	      echo "$(date -u +%H:%M:%S) gang up (wandb run exists), compile ceiling ${COMPILE_CEILING}s starts now"
   113	    fi
   114	  fi
   115	  if [ "${train_preemptions:-0}" -gt 0 ] 2>/dev/null; then
   116	    cancel_arm "train job preempted (preemptions=${train_preemptions})"
   117	    echo "WATCHDOG preempted train=${train_state} preemptions=${train_preemptions} step=${steps}"
   118	    exit 0
   119	  fi
   120	  if [ "${train_failures:-0}" -gt 0 ] 2>/dev/null; then
   121	    cancel_arm "train task failed (failures=${train_failures})"
   122	    echo "WATCHDOG task-failed train=${train_state} failures=${train_failures} step=${steps}"
   123	    exit 0
   124	  fi
   125	  case "${train_state:-}" in
   126	    succeeded|killed|cancelled|*failed)
   127	      echo "WATCHDOG terminal train=${train_state} step=${steps}"; exit 0;;
   128	  esac
   129	
   130	  if [ $(( tick % STEP_POLL_EVERY )) -eq 0 ]; then
   131	    steps="$(max_step)"
   132	    echo "$(date -u +%H:%M:%S) t=$(( now - start ))s train=${train_state:-none} step=${steps}"
   133	    # A restored arm resumes at the checkpoint's step, so progress is counted from the first step
   134	    # this watch actually observed rather than from zero.
   135	    if [ "${first_step:--1}" -lt 0 ] && [ "${steps:--1}" -ge 0 ] 2>/dev/null; then
   136	      first_step="$steps"
   137	      deadline=$(( now + STEP_BUDGET ))
   138	      echo "$(date -u +%H:%M:%S) first observed step=${first_step}, step budget ${STEP_BUDGET}s starts now"
   139	    fi
   140	    if [ "${first_step:--1}" -ge 0 ] 2>/dev/null && [ $(( steps - first_step )) -ge "$SCORE_MAX_STEP" ]; then
   141	      cancel_arm "scored through step ${steps} (${SCORE_MAX_STEP} past first)"
   142	      echo "WATCHDOG scored step=${steps} first=${first_step}"
   143	      exit 0
   144	    fi
   145	  fi
   146	  tick=$(( tick + 1 ))
   147	  sleep "$POLL"
   148	done
