codex
Stop-ship: the campaign can still silently score a non-restored run as valid. I would not spend rack time yet.

The files changed concurrently during review; findings below are against current HEAD `966b094ced`. I made no changes.

## Critical

1. Explicit restore is not mandatory and can silently fall back to step 0.

   [`launch_diagnostics.py:63`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/experiments/grug/moe_hero_ep/launch_diagnostics.py:63) accepts optional `restore_from`, but [`launch_diagnostics.py:194`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/experiments/grug/moe_hero_ep/launch_diagnostics.py:194) sets only `load_checkpoint_path`; it never sets `load_checkpoint=True`. The underlying restore helper treats `load_checkpoint=None` as optional and can return a fresh initialized state when no checkpoint is found.

   That failure is invisible to the protocol: [`score.py:53`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/score.py:53) defines the window relative to whatever first step exists, and [`watchdog.sh:135`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/watchdog.sh:135) accepts any nonnegative first step. A scratch run can therefore score steps 5–19 with `window_complete=true` despite never restoring step 30000.

   The scorer/watchdog must reject any campaign arm whose first logged training step is not 30000, and an explicit restore must be fail-required.

2. The watchdog is neither launched nor reliably addressable by `arm.sh`.

   [`arm.sh:53`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/arm.sh:53) submits with `--no-wait` and returns without starting `watchdog.sh`. Its hard caps therefore depend entirely on an unreviewed external harness.

   Additionally, [`arm.sh:59`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/arm.sh:59) passes `IRIS_USER=mwittmann` only into the remote environment; it does not select the top-level submission user. [`watchdog.sh:37`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/watchdog.sh:37) nevertheless hard-codes `/mwittmann/...`. If the caller’s local `IRIS_USER`/OS user differs, the watchdog observes and cancels nonexistent paths, leaving only the 3500-second outer timeout.

## High

3. The 1200-second post-compile cap is not hard.

   The deadline is checked only at [`watchdog.sh:76`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/watchdog.sh:76). A poll can then block for 120 seconds in `job describe`, 180 seconds in W&B, sleep another 20 seconds, and cancellation itself permits 300 seconds per command ([`watchdog.sh:54`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/watchdog.sh:54)). Rack release can materially exceed the deadline.

   There is also a re-arming bug: if the first run-existence probe transiently fails but the later step probe succeeds, the step deadline is set at [`watchdog.sh:137`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/watchdog.sh:137); on the next loop, [`watchdog.sh:105`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/watchdog.sh:105) can overwrite it with the longer 1800-second compile deadline.

4. Cancellation failures are reported as success.

   Both cancellation commands may fail or time out, but the script has no `set -e`, ignores their status, performs no terminal-state verification, prints a successful `WATCHDOG` marker, and exits zero ([`watchdog.sh:49`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/watchdog.sh:49), [`watchdog.sh:78`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/watchdog.sh:78)). A supervisor may submit the next arm while the gang is still alive.

5. RID/VERSION isolation is policy only, not enforced.

   [`arm.sh:22`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/arm.sh:22) accepts arbitrary IDs without checking W&B, Iris, artifact storage, or compile-cache existence. The same RID becomes:

   - the W&B ID, with Levanter’s `resume="allow"`;
   - the artifact name at [`launch_diagnostics.py:188`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/experiments/grug/moe_hero_ep/launch_diagnostics.py:188);
   - the compilation-cache directory at [`arm.sh:65`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/arm.sh:65).

   Reuse can merge old control/treatment rows, make the watchdog see stale completed steps, reuse an artifact without running, or collide with the live hero namespace. The comments and DESIGN warning do not prevent it.

6. A 15-step window cannot establish a +0.15 MFU keep from one A/B pair.

   The 15 measurements share placement, thermal state, neighboring workload, compilation history, and sequential autocorrelation; they are not 15 independent replicates. One control run and one treatment run provide no run-level variance estimate.

   Even under an optimistic independence assumption, detecting a 0.15 difference at roughly 95% confidence requires per-step SD below about `0.30 MFU`; positive autocorrelation lowers that limit substantially. `score.py` reports only a within-run SD, not paired per-step MFU differences or variation among run means.

   More importantly, [`DESIGN.md:90`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/DESIGN.md:90) now claims one rack eliminates placement variance, directly contradicting the supplied ±2pp cross-night constraint. Same-rack temporal placement is still placement. [`DESIGN.md:182`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/DESIGN.md:182) still permits a singular same-night pair to keep.

7. Guard completeness is reported but not enforced.

   [`score.py:74`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/score.py:74) validates only MFU count. Missing loss or drop rows merely produce short series or `None`; no overall validity flag fails. `loss_last` can still come from before `hi`, and NaN/Inf values are accepted by `float`, means, and JSON output. The newly emitted counts and loss series help manual review but do not prevent silent acceptance.

## Medium

8. H0’s profile tail conflicts with early release.

   H0 schedules profiling at absolute step 30021 ([`DESIGN.md:116`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/DESIGN.md:116)), while the fixed watchdog cancels at first-observed `+19` ([`watchdog.sh:140`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/watchdog.sh:140)). With first step 30000, cancellation happens at 30019, before the trace. H0/H1 require an explicit later `SCORE_MAX_STEP`; polling lag cannot be relied upon.

9. The hypothesis ledger contains contradictions or non-runnable entries.

   - H4 permits `drop_fraction_max <= 1e-3` ([`DESIGN.md:133`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/DESIGN.md:133)), while the fixed guard requires drops “not worse” than a roughly `5e-5` control ([`DESIGN.md:86`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/DESIGN.md:86)). It also cannot establish whole-run loss equivalence from 15 batches.
   - H5 is explicitly dead as an arm, H7 dead unless memory changes, and H8 unavailable on this branch/wheel ([`DESIGN.md:142`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/DESIGN.md:142), [`DESIGN.md:165`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/DESIGN.md:165)). They should not be treated as tonight’s executable hypothesis queue.
   - H2/H3 remain themes rather than treatments and do not yet specify the engagement checks required by [`DESIGN.md:13`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/DESIGN.md:13).

10. The dirty-tree guard exempts executable campaign protocol.

   [`arm.sh:31`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/arm.sh:31) excludes every `autoresearch/` modification, including `arm.sh`, `watchdog.sh`, and `score.py`, while [`arm.sh:84`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/arm.sh:84) records only HEAD. Future uncommitted protocol changes can therefore be attributed to the wrong commit.

## Verified non-findings

- With a unique RID/VERSION and successful restore, `--no-save-checkpoints` keeps writes under the arm artifact path; the hero checkpoint is read-only.
- The current `MASTER_PARAMS=device` value matches the launcher’s enum; the earlier `disabled` failure is fixed.
- The outer Iris timeout is attached to the single-task coordinator. Current Iris defaults such parents to terminate descendants, so a normal 3500-second timeout cascades to the 16-node child. Timeout scanning is minute-granularity, leaving only about 40 seconds of the nominal one-hour margin, but I did not find the previously alleged normal-path orphan.
- Bash syntax passes. ShellCheck reports only unused `coord_preemptions`.

Bottom line: require a provable step-30000 restore, make watchdog ownership/path/cancellation reliable, and establish replicated same-night blocked A/B inference before rack use.
tokens used
378,719
Stop-ship: the campaign can still silently score a non-restored run as valid. I would not spend rack time yet.

The files changed concurrently during review; findings below are against current HEAD `966b094ced`. I made no changes.

## Critical

1. Explicit restore is not mandatory and can silently fall back to step 0.

   [`launch_diagnostics.py:63`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/experiments/grug/moe_hero_ep/launch_diagnostics.py:63) accepts optional `restore_from`, but [`launch_diagnostics.py:194`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/experiments/grug/moe_hero_ep/launch_diagnostics.py:194) sets only `load_checkpoint_path`; it never sets `load_checkpoint=True`. The underlying restore helper treats `load_checkpoint=None` as optional and can return a fresh initialized state when no checkpoint is found.

   That failure is invisible to the protocol: [`score.py:53`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/score.py:53) defines the window relative to whatever first step exists, and [`watchdog.sh:135`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/watchdog.sh:135) accepts any nonnegative first step. A scratch run can therefore score steps 5–19 with `window_complete=true` despite never restoring step 30000.

   The scorer/watchdog must reject any campaign arm whose first logged training step is not 30000, and an explicit restore must be fail-required.

2. The watchdog is neither launched nor reliably addressable by `arm.sh`.

   [`arm.sh:53`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/arm.sh:53) submits with `--no-wait` and returns without starting `watchdog.sh`. Its hard caps therefore depend entirely on an unreviewed external harness.

   Additionally, [`arm.sh:59`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/arm.sh:59) passes `IRIS_USER=mwittmann` only into the remote environment; it does not select the top-level submission user. [`watchdog.sh:37`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/watchdog.sh:37) nevertheless hard-codes `/mwittmann/...`. If the caller’s local `IRIS_USER`/OS user differs, the watchdog observes and cancels nonexistent paths, leaving only the 3500-second outer timeout.

## High

3. The 1200-second post-compile cap is not hard.

   The deadline is checked only at [`watchdog.sh:76`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/watchdog.sh:76). A poll can then block for 120 seconds in `job describe`, 180 seconds in W&B, sleep another 20 seconds, and cancellation itself permits 300 seconds per command ([`watchdog.sh:54`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/watchdog.sh:54)). Rack release can materially exceed the deadline.

   There is also a re-arming bug: if the first run-existence probe transiently fails but the later step probe succeeds, the step deadline is set at [`watchdog.sh:137`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/watchdog.sh:137); on the next loop, [`watchdog.sh:105`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/watchdog.sh:105) can overwrite it with the longer 1800-second compile deadline.

4. Cancellation failures are reported as success.

   Both cancellation commands may fail or time out, but the script has no `set -e`, ignores their status, performs no terminal-state verification, prints a successful `WATCHDOG` marker, and exits zero ([`watchdog.sh:49`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/watchdog.sh:49), [`watchdog.sh:78`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/watchdog.sh:78)). A supervisor may submit the next arm while the gang is still alive.

5. RID/VERSION isolation is policy only, not enforced.

   [`arm.sh:22`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/arm.sh:22) accepts arbitrary IDs without checking W&B, Iris, artifact storage, or compile-cache existence. The same RID becomes:

   - the W&B ID, with Levanter’s `resume="allow"`;
   - the artifact name at [`launch_diagnostics.py:188`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/experiments/grug/moe_hero_ep/launch_diagnostics.py:188);
   - the compilation-cache directory at [`arm.sh:65`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/arm.sh:65).

   Reuse can merge old control/treatment rows, make the watchdog see stale completed steps, reuse an artifact without running, or collide with the live hero namespace. The comments and DESIGN warning do not prevent it.

6. A 15-step window cannot establish a +0.15 MFU keep from one A/B pair.

   The 15 measurements share placement, thermal state, neighboring workload, compilation history, and sequential autocorrelation; they are not 15 independent replicates. One control run and one treatment run provide no run-level variance estimate.

   Even under an optimistic independence assumption, detecting a 0.15 difference at roughly 95% confidence requires per-step SD below about `0.30 MFU`; positive autocorrelation lowers that limit substantially. `score.py` reports only a within-run SD, not paired per-step MFU differences or variation among run means.

   More importantly, [`DESIGN.md:90`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/DESIGN.md:90) now claims one rack eliminates placement variance, directly contradicting the supplied ±2pp cross-night constraint. Same-rack temporal placement is still placement. [`DESIGN.md:182`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/DESIGN.md:182) still permits a singular same-night pair to keep.

7. Guard completeness is reported but not enforced.

   [`score.py:74`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/score.py:74) validates only MFU count. Missing loss or drop rows merely produce short series or `None`; no overall validity flag fails. `loss_last` can still come from before `hi`, and NaN/Inf values are accepted by `float`, means, and JSON output. The newly emitted counts and loss series help manual review but do not prevent silent acceptance.

## Medium

8. H0’s profile tail conflicts with early release.

   H0 schedules profiling at absolute step 30021 ([`DESIGN.md:116`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/DESIGN.md:116)), while the fixed watchdog cancels at first-observed `+19` ([`watchdog.sh:140`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/watchdog.sh:140)). With first step 30000, cancellation happens at 30019, before the trace. H0/H1 require an explicit later `SCORE_MAX_STEP`; polling lag cannot be relied upon.

9. The hypothesis ledger contains contradictions or non-runnable entries.

   - H4 permits `drop_fraction_max <= 1e-3` ([`DESIGN.md:133`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/DESIGN.md:133)), while the fixed guard requires drops “not worse” than a roughly `5e-5` control ([`DESIGN.md:86`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/DESIGN.md:86)). It also cannot establish whole-run loss equivalence from 15 batches.
   - H5 is explicitly dead as an arm, H7 dead unless memory changes, and H8 unavailable on this branch/wheel ([`DESIGN.md:142`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/DESIGN.md:142), [`DESIGN.md:165`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/DESIGN.md:165)). They should not be treated as tonight’s executable hypothesis queue.
   - H2/H3 remain themes rather than treatments and do not yet specify the engagement checks required by [`DESIGN.md:13`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/DESIGN.md:13).

10. The dirty-tree guard exempts executable campaign protocol.

   [`arm.sh:31`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/arm.sh:31) excludes every `autoresearch/` modification, including `arm.sh`, `watchdog.sh`, and `score.py`, while [`arm.sh:84`](/home/marin/projects/marin-mfu-loop/autoresearch/loop-260828-8753mfu/arm.sh:84) records only HEAD.
